File: README.md

# 🔴 Pokédex Web Application

A modern, responsive, client-first Pokédex application built with vanilla JavaScript (ES modules), CSS3, and PokéAPI. The application features paginated directory loading, live search, dual-type and generation filtering, an interactive team builder with defensive synergy calculation, a PC Storage Box management drawer, camera/image-based Pokémon recognition via Google Lens, a "Who's That Pokémon?" mini-game, and Progressive Web App (PWA) installation support.

---

## 📑 Table of Contents

- [Key Features](#-key-features)
- [Architecture & Tech Stack](#-architecture--tech-stack)
- [Project Directory Structure](#-project-directory-structure)
- [Installation & Getting Started](#-installation--getting-started)
- [Progressive Web App (PWA) Support](#-progressive-web-app-pwa-support)

- [Camera Scanner & Serverless API](#-camera-scanner--serverless-api)

- [License](#-license)

---

## 🚀 Key Features

- **Progressive Web App (PWA) Installable:** Can be installed on desktop and mobile home screens as a standalone app while fetching live data online.
- **Infinite Scrolling & Batch Loading:** Loads Pokémon records in modular batches with client-side caching to reduce network overhead.
- **Dynamic Multi-Vector Filtering:** Filter seamlessly by primary type, secondary type, and Generation (Gen 1 through Gen 9).
- **Debounced Live Search:** Fast client-side lookups by Pokémon name, National Dex ID, alternate form tags, and base species.
- **Multiple Sprite Display Modes:** User-selectable sprite rendering modes (Official Artwork, 3D / Animated Sprites, and Retro Pixel Sprites).
- **Interactive Detail Modal:**
  - Dynamic base stats with Lv. 100 Min/Max boundary recalculations.
  - Interactive Shiny sprite toggle with custom particle animations.
  - Audio cries paired with browser Web Speech Synthesis (`SpeechSynthesisUtterance`).
  - Version-specific flavor texts and generation-filtered movepools (Level-up, TM/HM, Egg, Tutor).
  - Interactive multi-branch evolution trees supporting regional variants and Mega/Battle forms.
- **Defensive Type Matchups & Squad Synergy:** Real-time defensive vulnerability and resistance calculator across single cards and full 6-member teams.
- **Caught Pokémon (PC Box System):** Full desktop/mobile PC box interface with slide-out inspection drawer, Gen filters, sort toggles, and bulk release flows.
- **Camera Pokédex Scanner:** Live webcam or image upload scanner identifying Pokémon via a serverless Google Lens/SerpApi bridge.
- **"Who's That Pokémon?" Mini-Game:** Interactive silhouette guessing game featuring streak tracking, high scores, and audio feedback.

---

## 🛠 Architecture & Tech Stack

- **Frontend Core:** HTML5, Modern CSS3 (CSS Grid, Flexbox, Custom Properties, Glassmorphism), Vanilla JavaScript (ES6+ Modules).
- **PWA & Offline Shell:** Web App Manifest (`manifest.json`), Service Worker (`sw.js`).
- **Icons & Typography:** Font Awesome 6.5.1, Google Fonts (*Plus Jakarta Sans*).
- **Data Source:** [PokéAPI v2](https://pokeapi.co/) REST API.
- **Backend / Serverless Integration:** Node.js Serverless Function (`api/scan.js`) interacting with SerpApi (Google Lens engine).
- **State Management:** Local browser persistence via `window.localStorage` combined with an in-memory `Map` cache.

---

## 📂 Project Directory Structure

```text
mhelric-pokedex/
├── README.md                 # Project documentation
├── index.html                # Single-page application markup & modal templates
├── manifest.json             # Web App Manifest for PWA installation
├── sw.js                     # Service Worker for app shell caching & network requests
├── icons/                    # App icons (192x192, 512x512, favicons)
│   ├── icon-192.png
│   └── icon-512.png
├── api/
│   └── scan.js               # Serverless endpoint for reverse image search (Lens/SerpApi)
├── js/
│   ├── api.js                # PokéAPI client wrappers, data transforms & calculations
│   ├── app.js                # App entry point, DOM event delegation & state orchestration
│   ├── minigame.js           # Trivia mini-game loop, streak handler & scoring logic
│   ├── scanner.js            # MediaDevices camera feed capture & image serialization
│   ├── team.js               # Team builder roster state & storage sync
│   └── ui.js                 # HTML string template builders & DOM render functions
└── styles/
    ├── animations.css        # Keyframe animations (sparkles, Pokeball loader, scan laser)
    ├── main.css              # Core typography, reset, grid layouts & design tokens
    └── retro-ui.css          # Theme overrides, modal layouts & PC box styling

```

---

## 📦 Installation & Getting Started

### Prerequisites

* Modern Web Browser (Chrome, Safari, Firefox, or Edge supporting WebRTC, Service Workers, and ES Modules).
* [Node.js](https://nodejs.org/) (v18.x or later recommended if testing the `api/scan.js` serverless function locally).

### Local Setup

1. **Clone the repository:**
```bash
git clone [https://github.com/your-username/mhelric-pokedex.git](https://github.com/your-username/mhelric-pokedex.git)
cd mhelric-pokedex

```


2. **Serve the client application:**
Because the project relies on native ES Modules and Service Workers, it must be served over an HTTP server rather than the `file://` protocol.
Using Node's built-in `npx serve`:
```bash
npx serve .

```


Or using Python 3:
```bash
python3 -m http.server 8000

```


Open `http://localhost:8000` (or the URL printed in terminal) in your browser.

---

## 📲 Progressive Web App (PWA) Support

This application meets all Chromium criteria for an installable Progressive Web App:

* **Standalone Display:** Runs in a distraction-free window without URL chrome when installed.
* **In-App Install Prompt:** Captures `beforeinstallprompt` to surface a clean install button inside the Settings modal.
* **Network-First Service Worker:** Employs a network-first caching strategy in `sw.js` to ensure the live app shell loads reliably while always fetching up-to-date PokéAPI records from the web.

---

## 🔐 Environment Variables

The visual scanner feature (`api/scan.js`) utilizes SerpApi to access the Google Lens reverse search engine. If deploying to **Vercel**, **Netlify**, or another serverless host, set the following environment variable:

| Variable | Description | Required |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `SERPAPI_KEY` | Your private SerpApi authentication key | Yes (for camera scanner only) |

*Note: All core Pokédex browsing, searching, minigame, and team builder features function completely client-side without this key.*

---

## 🧩 Core Modules Breakdown

### `js/app.js` (Orchestrator)

Initializes UI controls, manages active filter states (`activeType`, `activeGen`, `spriteDisplayMode`), listens for modal closures, registers the PWA install flow, and controls the infinite scroll lifecycle via `loadScrollBatch()`. Uses native `AbortController` to cancel pending background requests during rapid filter changes.

### `sw.js` (Service Worker)

Manages the application lifecycle, pre-caches core layout assets (HTML, stylesheets, scripts) on install, and routes runtime network requests dynamically.

### `js/ui.js` (Component Rendering)

Encapsulates rendering logic for Pokémon grid cards, modal contents, stat bars, movepool tables, evolution trees, and defensive synergy tables. Calculates and normalizes min/max Pokémon stats based on Lv. 100 EV/IV spreads.

### `js/team.js` (Roster Store)

Enforces a hard limit of 6 squad members with duplicate detection. Synchronizes roster operations (`addToTeam`, `removeFromTeam`, `clearTeam`) with `localStorage`.

### `js/scanner.js` & `api/scan.js` (Image Recognition)

Accesses camera hardware via `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia`, extracts frames onto a hidden canvas, and converts them to base64 JPEG buffers. The backend parses Google Lens candidates against PokéAPI endpoints to return identified species names.

### `js/minigame.js` (Game Loop)

Fetches 4 random Pokémon records concurrently, generates silhouette CSS filters, evaluates user picks, plays Pokémon audio cries, and maintains win-streak data.

---

## 💾 Storage & Persistence Architecture

The application persists user settings and rosters across browser sessions using `localStorage`:

| Key Pattern | Type | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `pokedex_team` | `Array<Object>` | Serialized array of Pokémon in the active 6-member squad. |
| `caught_<id>` | `Boolean` | Tracking state indicating whether Pokémon ID is caught. |
| `fav_<id>` | `Boolean` | Favorited status of Pokémon ID. |
| `sprite_display_mode` | `String` | Active sprite view preference (`artwork`, `animated`, or `pixel`). |
| `minigame_highscore` | `Number` | Persistent high score record for the silhouette game. |

---

## ⚡ Performance & Optimization Strategies

* **In-Memory Caching:** Uses an internal `fetchedCache` Map to prevent duplicate network calls for already fetched Pokémon objects.
* **Debounced Input:** User search queries are throttled with a 350ms debounce function to prevent API rate-limiting.
* **Abortable Signals:** Uses `AbortController` instances to cancel in-flight batch requests if the user switches filters mid-fetch.
* **Lazy Asset Loading:** All card and evolution thumbnails specify `loading="lazy"` to defer image decoding until elements enter the viewport.

---

File: sw.js
================================================
// sw.js
const SHELL_CACHE = "pokedex-shell-v8";
const API_CACHE = "pokedex-api-v1";
const MAX_API_ENTRIES = 120;

const STATIC_ASSETS = [
  "./",
  "./index.html",
  "./manifest.json",
  "./styles/main.css",
  "./styles/retro-ui.css",
  "./styles/themes.css",
  "./styles/animations.css",
  "./js/app.js",
  "./js/api.js",
  "./js/ui.js",
  "./js/team.js",
  "./js/storage.js",
  "./js/minigame.js",
  "./js/scanner.js",
  "./js/utils.js",
  "./icons/pokedex-icon-192.png",
  "./icons/pokedex-icon-512.png"
];

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(SHELL_CACHE).then(async (cache) => {
      for (const asset of STATIC_ASSETS) {
        try {
          await cache.add(asset);
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn(`[SW] Precache skipped for: ${asset}`, err);
        }
      }
    })
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .keys()
      .then((keys) =>
        Promise.all(
          keys.map((key) => {
            if (key !== SHELL_CACHE && key !== API_CACHE) {
              return caches.delete(key);
            }
          })
        )
      )
      .then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

async function trimCache(cacheName, maxItems) {
  const cache = await caches.open(cacheName);
  const keys = await cache.keys();
  if (keys.length > maxItems) {
    await cache.delete(keys[0]);
    trimCache(cacheName, maxItems);
  }
}

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  if (event.request.method !== "GET") return;

  const url = new URL(event.request.url);

  // Bypass service worker for external CDN image and asset domains so browser fetches directly
  if (
    url.origin.includes("jsdelivr.net") ||
    url.origin.includes("githubusercontent.com") ||
    url.origin.includes("pokemonshowdown.com") ||
    url.origin.includes("cloudflare.com") ||
    url.origin.includes("googleapis.com") ||
    url.origin.includes("gstatic.com")
  ) {
    return;
  }

  // 1. Cross-origin PokéAPI cache
  if (url.origin.includes("pokeapi.co")) {
    event.respondWith(
      caches.open(API_CACHE).then(async (cache) => {
        try {
          const networkResponse = await fetch(event.request);
          if (networkResponse.ok) {
            cache.put(event.request, networkResponse.clone());
            trimCache(API_CACHE, MAX_API_ENTRIES);
          }
          return networkResponse;
        } catch {
          const cached = await cache.match(event.request.url, { ignoreSearch: true });
          if (cached) return cached;
          return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Offline" }), {
            status: 503,
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          });
        }
      })
    );
    return;
  }

  // 2. Navigation & Static Assets: Network-First with Cache Fallback
  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request)
      .then((networkResponse) => {
        if (networkResponse && networkResponse.ok) {
          const clone = networkResponse.clone();
          caches.open(SHELL_CACHE).then((cache) => cache.put(event.request, clone));
        }
        return networkResponse;
      })
      .catch(async () => {
        const cached = await caches.match(event.request.url, { ignoreSearch: true });
        if (cached) return cached;

        if (event.request.mode === "navigate") {
          const shellFallback =
            (await caches.match("./index.html")) ||
            (await caches.match("/index.html")) ||
            (await caches.match("./")) ||
            (await caches.match("/"));
          if (shellFallback) return shellFallback;
        }

        return new Response("Resource unavailable offline", { status: 503 });
      })
  );
});

// api/scan.js

import { CANONICAL_SPECIES } from "./_species.js";

let speciesCache = null;
const ipHits = new Map(); // In-memory rate limiting stub. In production, use Upstash / Vercel KV.

// Species aliases for common abbreviations, punctuation differences, or gender distinctions
const SPECIES_ALIASES = {
  "nidoran": "nidoran-m",
  "nidoran-f": "nidoran-f",
  "nidoran-m": "nidoran-m",
  "nidoran-female": "nidoran-f",
  "nidoran-male": "nidoran-m",
  "farfetchd": "farfetchd",
  "sirfetchd": "sirfetchd",
  "mr-mime": "mr-mime",
  "mime-jr": "mime-jr",
  "mr-rime": "mr-rime",
  "great-tusk": "great-tusk",
  "scream-tail": "scream-tail",
  "brute-bonnet": "brute-bonnet",
  "flutter-mane": "flutter-mane",
  "slither-wing": "slither-wing",
  "sandy-shocks": "sandy-shocks",
  "iron-treads": "iron-treads",
  "iron-bundle": "iron-bundle",
  "iron-hands": "iron-hands",
  "iron-jugulis": "iron-jugulis",
  "iron-moth": "iron-moth",
  "iron-thorns": "iron-thorns",
  "roaring-moon": "roaring-moon",
  "iron-valiant": "iron-valiant",
  "walking-wake": "walking-wake",
  "iron-leaves": "iron-leaves",
  "gouging-fire": "gouging-fire",
  "raging-bolt": "raging-bolt",
  "iron-boulder": "iron-boulder",
  "iron-crown": "iron-crown",
  "wo-chien": "wo-chien",
  "chien-pao": "chien-pao",
  "ting-lu": "ting-lu",
  "chi-yu": "chi-yu",
  "type-null": "type-null",
  "tapu-koko": "tapu-koko",
  "tapu-lele": "tapu-lele",
  "tapu-bulu": "tapu-bulu",
  "tapu-fini": "tapu-fini"
};

/**
 * Returns known Pokémon species names.
 * Immediately uses CANONICAL_SPECIES without cold-start blocking.
 * Lazily refreshes speciesCache in the background if newer species exist.
 */
async function getSpeciesNames() {
  if (speciesCache && speciesCache.size > 0) return speciesCache;

  // Use the canonical offline set immediately
  speciesCache = new Set(CANONICAL_SPECIES);

  // Background non-blocking fetch to catch any future PokeAPI updates
  fetch("https://pokeapi.co/api/v2/pokemon-species?limit=2000")
    .then(async (res) => {
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        if (Array.isArray(data.results)) {
          data.results.forEach((r) => speciesCache.add(r.name.toLowerCase()));
        }
      }
    })
    .catch(() => {
      // Offline or network error: silently ignored since canonical set is already populated
    });

  return speciesCache;
}

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  if (req.method === "HEAD") {
    return res.status(200).end();
  }

  if (req.method !== "POST") {
    return res.status(405).json({ error: "Method not allowed" });
  }

  // Security check: Origin / Referer validation
  const origin = req.headers.origin || req.headers.referer || "";
  const host = req.headers.host || "";
  const isDev =
    process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" ||
    host.includes("localhost") ||
    host.includes("127.0.0.1") ||
    host.includes("0.0.0.0");

  if (!isDev) {
    if (!origin) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden: Origin or Referer header required" });
    }
    const originHost = (origin.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").split("/")[0] || "").split(":")[0];
    const expectedHost = host.split(":")[0];
    if (originHost !== expectedHost && !originHost.endsWith(".vercel.app")) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden origin" });
    }
  } else {
    // In local dev, allow localhost/127.0.0.1, but reject external mismatches if an origin is provided
    if (
      origin &&
      host &&
      !origin.includes("localhost") &&
      !origin.includes("127.0.0.1") &&
      !origin.includes(host)
    ) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden origin" });
    }
  }

  // Rate limiting (max 15 scans per minute per IP)
  const clientIp =
    req.headers["x-forwarded-for"]?.split(",")[0]?.trim() ||
    req.socket?.remoteAddress ||
    "local";
  const now = Date.now();
  const windowMs = 60 * 1000;

  // Prune expired IPs if cache grows large to prevent memory leaks
  if (ipHits.size > 500) {
    for (const [ip, timestamps] of ipHits.entries()) {
      const active = timestamps.filter((t) => now - t < windowMs);
      if (active.length === 0) {
        ipHits.delete(ip);
      } else {
        ipHits.set(ip, active);
      }
    }
  }

  const history = (ipHits.get(clientIp) || []).filter((time) => now - time < windowMs);
  if (history.length >= 15) {
    return res.status(429).json({ error: "Scan limit exceeded. Please wait a minute." });
  }
  history.push(now);
  ipHits.set(clientIp, history);

  try {
    const { image } = req.body || {};
    if (!image || typeof image !== "string") {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Valid base64 image data is required." });
    }

    // Payload size cap: max 1.5MB base64
    if (image.length > 1.5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return res.status(413).json({ error: "Image exceeds 1.5MB limit." });
    }

    // Validate prefix
    const dataUrlMatch = image.match(/^data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,/);
    if (!dataUrlMatch) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Image must be a valid JPEG, PNG, or WebP base64 data URL." });
    }

    const serpApiKey = process.env.SERPAPI_KEY;
    if (!serpApiKey) {
      return res.status(500).json({ error: "SERPAPI_KEY environment variable is missing." });
    }

    const base64Data = image.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
    const imageBuffer = Buffer.from(base64Data, "base64");

    // Upload to SerpApi
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("image", new Blob([imageBuffer], { type: "image/jpeg" }), "scan.jpg");
    formData.append("api_key", serpApiKey);

    const uploadResponse = await fetch("https://serpapi.com/image", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    });

    if (!uploadResponse.ok) {
      throw new Error(`SerpApi image upload failed with status ${uploadResponse.status}`);
    }

    const uploadData = await uploadResponse.json();
    const imageId = uploadData.image_id;
    if (!imageId) {
      throw new Error("Failed to obtain image_id from SerpApi.");
    }

    // Search Google Lens
    const lensUrl = `https://serpapi.com/search.json?engine=google_lens&image_id=${encodeURIComponent(
      imageId
    )}&hl=en&api_key=${serpApiKey}`;

    const lensResponse = await fetch(lensUrl);
    if (!lensResponse.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Google Lens search failed with status ${lensResponse.status}`);
    }

    const lensData = await lensResponse.json();
    const candidateTexts = [];

    if (lensData.knowledge_graph?.title) {
      candidateTexts.push(lensData.knowledge_graph.title);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(lensData.visual_matches)) {
      lensData.visual_matches.slice(0, 12).forEach((item) => {
        if (item.title) candidateTexts.push(item.title);
      });
    }

    const knownSpecies = await getSpeciesNames();
    const stopWords = new Set([
      "pokemon", "the", "card", "plush", "plushie", "figure", "toy", "gx", "ex",
      "vmax", "vstar", "tcg", "holo", "rare", "ultra", "shiny", "edition", "series",
      "nintendo", "game", "freak", "scarlet", "violet", "sword", "shield", "pocket",
      "monsters", "japan", "japanese", "english", "mint", "graded", "promo"
    ]);

    let detectedPokemon = null;

    // Scan n-grams (2-word phrases like "mr mime" or "iron valiant", then single words)
    for (const text of candidateTexts) {
      const words = text
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/♀/g, " female")
        .replace(/♂/g, " male")
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s-]/g, "")
        .split(/\s+/)
        .filter((w) => w && !stopWords.has(w) && !/^\d+$/.test(w)); // Ignore purely numeric tokens

      // 2-word n-grams
      for (let i = 0; i < words.length - 1; i++) {
        const biGram = `${words[i]}-${words[i + 1]}`;
        const matched = SPECIES_ALIASES[biGram] || (knownSpecies.has(biGram) ? biGram : null);
        if (matched) {
          detectedPokemon = matched;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (detectedPokemon) break;

      // 1-word tokens
      for (const word of words) {
        if (word.length > 2) {
          const matched = SPECIES_ALIASES[word] || (knownSpecies.has(word) ? word : null);
          if (matched) {
            detectedPokemon = matched;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (detectedPokemon) break;
    }

    if (!detectedPokemon) {
      return res.status(200).json({ pokemon: "none" });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({ pokemon: detectedPokemon });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Reverse Image Search Error:", error);
    return res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || "Reverse Image Search failed" });
  }
}

// ==========================================
// UTILITY HELPERS
// ==========================================

/**
 * Escapes unsafe characters in dynamic text before inserting into HTML templates.
 * @param {string|number} str
 * @returns {string}
 */
export function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (str === null || str === undefined) return "";
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

File: js/storage.js

// ==========================================
// STORAGE MODULE
// Unified LocalStorage management for Caught & Favorite Pokémon collections
// ==========================================

const CAUGHT_STORAGE_KEY = "pokedex_caught";
const FAV_STORAGE_KEY = "pokedex_favorites";

// In-memory sets for O(1) membership lookups
let caughtSet = new Set();
let favSet = new Set();

/**
 * Migrates legacy individual localStorage keys (e.g., "caught_25", "fav_1")
 * into unified JSON arrays under "pokedex_caught" and "pokedex_favorites".
 */
function initializeAndMigrateStorage() {
  try {
    // 1. Load existing unified storage if present
    const savedCaught = localStorage.getItem(CAUGHT_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (savedCaught) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(savedCaught);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          parsed.forEach((id) => {
            const num = Number(id);
            if (!isNaN(num) && num > 0) caughtSet.add(num);
          });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Error parsing unified caught storage:", err);
      }
    }

    const savedFav = localStorage.getItem(FAV_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (savedFav) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(savedFav);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          parsed.forEach((id) => {
            const num = Number(id);
            if (!isNaN(num) && num > 0) favSet.add(num);
          });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Error parsing unified favorites storage:", err);
      }
    }

    // 2. Scan for and migrate legacy individual keys
    const legacyKeysToRemove = [];
    let hadLegacyCaught = false;
    let hadLegacyFav = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
      const key = localStorage.key(i);
      if (!key) continue;

      if (key.startsWith("caught_")) {
        legacyKeysToRemove.push(key);
        if (localStorage.getItem(key) === "true") {
          const id = Number(key.slice(7));
          if (!isNaN(id) && id > 0) {
            caughtSet.add(id);
            hadLegacyCaught = true;
          }
        }
      } else if (key.startsWith("fav_")) {
        legacyKeysToRemove.push(key);
        if (localStorage.getItem(key) === "true") {
          const id = Number(key.slice(4));
          if (!isNaN(id) && id > 0) {
            favSet.add(id);
            hadLegacyFav = true;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Clean up legacy keys
    if (legacyKeysToRemove.length > 0) {
      legacyKeysToRemove.forEach((key) => localStorage.removeItem(key));
    }

    // 4. Persist unified representations if migrations took place
    if (hadLegacyCaught || (!savedCaught && caughtSet.size > 0)) {
      persistCaught();
    }
    if (hadLegacyFav || (!savedFav && favSet.size > 0)) {
      persistFavorites();
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Storage initialization error:", err);
  }
}

function persistCaught() {
  try {
    const list = Array.from(caughtSet).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    localStorage.setItem(CAUGHT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Failed to persist caught Pokémon to localStorage:", err);
  }
}

function persistFavorites() {
  try {
    const list = Array.from(favSet).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    localStorage.setItem(FAV_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Failed to persist favorite Pokémon to localStorage:", err);
  }
}

// Run initial migration and load immediately on module evaluation
initializeAndMigrateStorage();

// ==========================================
// CAUGHT POKÉMON API
// ==========================================

export function isPokemonCaught(id) {
  const num = Number(id);
  return !isNaN(num) && caughtSet.has(num);
}

export function setPokemonCaught(id, isCaught) {
  const num = Number(id);
  if (isNaN(num) || num <= 0) return false;

  if (isCaught) {
    caughtSet.add(num);
  } else {
    caughtSet.delete(num);
  }
  persistCaught();
  return isCaught;
}

export function togglePokemonCaught(id) {
  const num = Number(id);
  if (isNaN(num) || num <= 0) return false;

  const newState = !caughtSet.has(num);
  if (newState) {
    caughtSet.add(num);
  } else {
    caughtSet.delete(num);
  }
  persistCaught();
  return newState;
}

export function getCaughtIds() {
  return Array.from(caughtSet).sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

export function getCaughtCount() {
  return caughtSet.size;
}

export function clearAllCaught() {
  caughtSet.clear();
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(CAUGHT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify([]));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Failed to clear caught storage:", err);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// TEAM BUILDER STATE & STORAGE
// ==========================================

import { getCachedPokemon, fetchPokemonSummary } from "./api.js";

const STORAGE_KEY = "pokedex_team";

// In-memory team state stores numeric Pokémon IDs only
let teamIds = loadTeamIdsFromStorage();

/**
 * Retrieves and validates team IDs from localStorage, migrating legacy full objects.
 */
function loadTeamIdsFromStorage() {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return [];

    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];

    let migrated = false;
    const ids = [];

    for (const item of parsed) {
      const id = typeof item === "object" && item !== null ? Number(item.id) : Number(item);
      if (typeof item === "object" && item !== null) migrated = true;
      if (!isNaN(id) && id > 0 && !ids.includes(id) && ids.length < 6) {
        ids.push(id);
      }
    }

    if (migrated) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(ids));
    }
    return ids;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error reading team from storage:", error);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Syncs the current team IDs to localStorage.
 */
function saveTeamToStorage() {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(teamIds));
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error saving team to storage:", error);
  }
}

/**
 * Returns raw array of team member IDs.
 */
export function getTeamIds() {
  return [...teamIds];
}

/**
 * Hydrates any uncached team IDs via fetchPokemonSummary and returns summary objects.
 */
export async function hydrateTeam(signal = null) {
  const results = await Promise.all(
    teamIds.map((id) =>
      fetchPokemonSummary(id, signal).catch((err) => {
        if (err.name === "AbortError") throw err;
        console.warn(`Could not hydrate team member ${id}:`, err);
        return null;
      })
    )
  );
  return results.filter(Boolean);
}

/**
 * Returns an array of hydrated Pokémon summaries currently available in cache.
 */
export function getTeam() {
  return teamIds.map((id) => getCachedPokemon(id)).filter(Boolean);
}

/**
 * Adds a Pokémon by object or ID if the team is below 6 members and no duplicates exist.
 */
export function addToTeam(pokemonOrId) {
  const id = typeof pokemonOrId === "object" ? Number(pokemonOrId.id) : Number(pokemonOrId);
  const name =
    (typeof pokemonOrId === "object" && pokemonOrId.name) ||
    getCachedPokemon(id)?.name ||
    `#${id}`;

  if (teamIds.length >= 6) {
    return { success: false, message: "Your team is full! (Max 6 Pokémon)" };
  }

  if (teamIds.includes(id)) {
    return { success: false, message: `${name} is already in your team!` };
  }

  teamIds = [...teamIds, id];
  saveTeamToStorage();

  return { success: true, message: `Added ${name} to your team!` };
}

/**
 * Removes a Pokémon from the team by ID.
 */
export function removeFromTeam(pokemonId) {
  const numId = Number(pokemonId);
  teamIds = teamIds.filter((id) => id !== numId);
  saveTeamToStorage();
}

/**
 * Clears all members from the team.
 */
export function clearTeam() {
  teamIds = [];
  saveTeamToStorage();
}

// ==========================================
// MINI-GAME ENGINE ("Who's That Pokémon?")
// ==========================================

import { fetchPokemonMinigame } from "./api.js";

let currentTarget = null;
let currentStreak = 0;
let highScore = Number(localStorage.getItem("minigame_highscore")) || 0;
let minigameRoundToken = 0;

/**
 * Generates a random National Pokédex ID.
 */
function getRandomId(min = 1, max = 1025) {
  return Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;
}

/**
 * Unbiased in-place Fisher–Yates shuffle (returns a new shuffled copy).
 */
export function fisherYatesShuffle(arr) {
  const copy = [...arr];
  for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];
  }
  return copy;
}

/**
 * Sets up a new round with token guard against modal reopen race conditions.
 */
export async function startNewRound(
  imgElement,
  optionsContainer,
  feedbackElement,
  streakElem,
  highScoreElem
) {
  const roundToken = ++minigameRoundToken;
  const gameModal = document.getElementById("game-modal");
  const shutter = document.getElementById("game-shutter");

  // 1. Reset stage image and snap shutter shut immediately
  imgElement.className = "silhouette";
  imgElement.src = "";

  if (shutter) {
    shutter.classList.add("instant-close");
    shutter.classList.remove("open");
    shutter.style.visibility = "visible";
    shutter.style.opacity = "1";
    void shutter.offsetHeight;
  }

  feedbackElement.textContent = "Who's That Pokémon?";
  feedbackElement.style.color = "";

  // 2. Display loader in options grid
  optionsContainer.innerHTML = `
    <div class="game-options-loader">
      <div class="pokeball-spinner"></div>
      <span>Calling Pokémon...</span>
    </div>
  `;

  if (streakElem) streakElem.textContent = currentStreak;
  if (highScoreElem) highScoreElem.textContent = highScore;

  try {
    // 3. Select 4 unique IDs
    const ids = new Set();
    while (ids.size < 4) {
      ids.add(getRandomId(1, 1025));
    }

    const pokemonList = await Promise.all(
      Array.from(ids).map((id) => fetchPokemonMinigame(id))
    );

    // Abort if stale or modal was closed
    if (roundToken !== minigameRoundToken || gameModal?.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      return;
    }

    currentTarget = pokemonList[0];
    const choices = fisherYatesShuffle(pokemonList);
    const targetSrc = currentTarget.officialArtwork || currentTarget.image;

    // 4. Preload artwork buffer before lifting shutter
    await new Promise((resolve) => {
      const preloader = new Image();
      preloader.src = targetSrc;
      preloader.onload = resolve;
      preloader.onerror = resolve;
    });

    if (roundToken !== minigameRoundToken || gameModal?.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      return;
    }

    imgElement.src = targetSrc;

    // 5. Open shutter
    if (shutter) {
      shutter.classList.remove("instant-close");
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (roundToken === minigameRoundToken) {
          shutter.classList.add("open");
        }
      }, 120);
    }

    // 6. Render choice buttons
    optionsContainer.innerHTML = choices
      .map(
        (p) =>
          `<button class="option-btn" data-name="${p.name}">${p.name.toUpperCase()}</button>`
      )
      .join("");
  } catch (error) {
    if (roundToken === minigameRoundToken && !gameModal?.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      feedbackElement.textContent = "Failed to load mini-game round. Try again!";
      if (shutter) {
        shutter.classList.remove("instant-close");
        shutter.classList.add("open");
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Evaluates the chosen answer, reveals the sprite, plays audio cry, and updates streaks.
 */
export function handleGuess(
  selectedName,
  imgElement,
  feedbackElement,
  optionsContainer,
  nextBtn,
  streakElem,
  highScoreElem
) {
  if (!currentTarget) return;
  const isCorrect = selectedName.toLowerCase() === currentTarget.name.toLowerCase();

  imgElement.classList.add("revealed");

  if (currentTarget?.cry) {
    const audio = new Audio(currentTarget.cry);
    audio.volume = 0.6;
    audio.play().catch((err) => console.error("Audio playback error:", err));
  }

  const buttons = optionsContainer.querySelectorAll(".option-btn");
  buttons.forEach((btn) => {
    btn.disabled = true;
    if (btn.dataset.name.toLowerCase() === currentTarget.name.toLowerCase()) {
      btn.classList.add("correct");
    } else if (btn.dataset.name.toLowerCase() === selectedName.toLowerCase() && !isCorrect) {
      btn.classList.add("wrong");
    }
  });

  if (isCorrect) {
    currentStreak++;
    if (currentStreak > highScore) {
      highScore = currentStreak;
      localStorage.setItem("minigame_highscore", highScore);
    }
    feedbackElement.textContent = `🎉 IT'S ${currentTarget.name.toUpperCase()}! Great guess!`;
    feedbackElement.style.color = "#4caf50";
  } else {
    currentStreak = 0;
    feedbackElement.textContent = `❌ It was ${currentTarget.name.toUpperCase()}! Streak reset.`;
    feedbackElement.style.color = "#ef5350";
  }

  if (streakElem) streakElem.textContent = currentStreak;
  if (highScoreElem) highScoreElem.textContent = highScore;

  if (nextBtn) nextBtn.classList.add("visible");
}

// ==========================================
// CAMERA POKÉDEX SCANNER MODULE (GOOGLE LENS VIA SERPAPI)
// ==========================================

import { fetchPokemonSummary } from "./api.js";

let mediaStream = null;

export async function checkScannerAvailability() {
  const scanBtn = document.getElementById("nav-scan-btn");
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/scan", { method: "HEAD" });
    if (res.status === 404 && scanBtn) {
      scanBtn.style.display = "none";
    }
  } catch {
    // If running purely offline or without backend, fail gracefully
  }
}

export async function startCameraStream(videoElement, statusElement) {
  stopCameraStream();

  const scannerModal = document.getElementById("scanner-modal");
  if (!scannerModal || scannerModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
    return;
  }

  try {
    statusElement.textContent = "Requesting camera access...";
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      video: { facingMode: "environment", width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 } },
      audio: false,
    });

    // Check if modal was closed while user prompt was active
    if (!scannerModal || scannerModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      return;
    }

    mediaStream = stream;
    videoElement.srcObject = mediaStream;
    await videoElement.play();
    statusElement.textContent = "Point camera at any Pokémon photo, card, or plushie and tap Scan!";
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Camera access error:", error);
    statusElement.textContent = "⚠️ Camera unavailable. Upload an image file below.";
  }
}

export function stopCameraStream() {
  if (mediaStream) {
    mediaStream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
    mediaStream = null;
  }
}

/**
 * Resizes an image or video frame to a max dimension of 800px at ~0.7 JPEG quality.
 */
function downscaleCanvasToDataUrl(source, naturalWidth, naturalHeight, maxDim = 800, quality = 0.7) {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  let width = naturalWidth;
  let height = naturalHeight;

  if (width > maxDim || height > maxDim) {
    if (width > height) {
      height = Math.round((height * maxDim) / width);
      width = maxDim;
    } else {
      width = Math.round((width * maxDim) / height);
      height = maxDim;
    }
  }

  canvas.width = width;
  canvas.height = height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  ctx.drawImage(source, 0, 0, width, height);
  return canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", quality);
}

async function classifyWithGoogleLens(base64Image, statusElement) {
  const scanBtn = document.getElementById("capture-scan-btn");
  if (scanBtn) scanBtn.disabled = true;

  statusElement.textContent = "🔍 Reverse searching with Google Lens...";

  try {
    const response = await fetch("/api/scan", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ image: base64Image }),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(errData.error || `Server error ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    const pokemonName = data.pokemon;

    if (!pokemonName || pokemonName === "none") {
      statusElement.textContent = "❓ No Pokémon detected. Center the target and try again!";
      return null;
    }

    statusElement.textContent = `🎯 Identified: ${pokemonName.toUpperCase()}! Fetching Dex Entry...`;
    return await fetchPokemonSummary(pokemonName);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Scanner Error:", err);
    statusElement.textContent = `❌ Scan failed: ${err.message}`;
    return null;
  } finally {
    if (scanBtn) scanBtn.disabled = false;
  }
}

export async function captureAndScanFrame(videoElement, canvasElement, statusElement) {
  if (!videoElement || !videoElement.videoWidth) {
    statusElement.textContent = "⚠️ Camera feed not ready.";
    return null;
  }

  const downscaledBase64 = downscaleCanvasToDataUrl(
    videoElement,
    videoElement.videoWidth,
    videoElement.videoHeight,
    800,
    0.7
  );

  return await classifyWithGoogleLens(downscaledBase64, statusElement);
}

export async function scanUploadedFile(fileObject, statusElement) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = async () => {
        const downscaledBase64 = downscaleCanvasToDataUrl(img, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight, 800, 0.7);
        const scannedPokemon = await classifyWithGoogleLens(downscaledBase64, statusElement);
        resolve(scannedPokemon);
      };
      img.onerror = () => {
        statusElement.textContent = "⚠️ Failed to process image file.";
        resolve(null);
      };
      img.src = reader.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = () => {
      statusElement.textContent = "⚠️ Failed to read uploaded file.";
      resolve(null);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(fileObject);
  });
}

// ==========================================
// POKÉAPI SERVICE
// Handles fetching, transforming & registry index
// ==========================================

const API = "https://pokeapi.co/api/v2";
const BASE_URL = `${API}/pokemon`;
const SPECIES_URL = `${API}/pokemon-species`;

const DIRECTORY_CACHE_KEY = "pokedex_directory_v2";
const DIRECTORY_CACHE_TTL = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 7 days

export const fetchedCache = new Map();
const inFlightRequests = new Map();

export function getCachedPokemon(nameOrId) {
  if (nameOrId === null || nameOrId === undefined) return null;
  const num = Number(nameOrId);
  if (!isNaN(num) && fetchedCache.has(num)) {
    return fetchedCache.get(num);
  }
  const key = String(nameOrId).toLowerCase().trim();
  return fetchedCache.get(key) || null;
}

export function setCachedPokemon(pokemon) {
  if (!pokemon) return;
  if (pokemon.id) {
    fetchedCache.set(Number(pokemon.id), pokemon);
  }
  if (pokemon.rawName) {
    fetchedCache.set(pokemon.rawName.toLowerCase(), pokemon);
  }
  if (pokemon.name) {
    fetchedCache.set(pokemon.name.toLowerCase(), pokemon);
  }
}

function sleep(ms, signal = null) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      return reject(signal.reason || new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(resolve, ms);
    if (signal) {
      signal.addEventListener(
        "abort",
        () => {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          reject(signal.reason || new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
        },
        { once: true }
      );
    }
  });
}

async function fetchJsonWithRetry(url, maxRetries = 3) {
  let attempt = 0;
  while (true) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {
        const err = new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} for ${url}`);
        err.status = response.status;
        err.retryable = true;
        throw err;
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        const err = new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} for ${url}`);
        err.status = response.status;
        err.retryable = false;
        throw err;
      }
      return await response.json();
    } catch (err) {
      const isNetworkError = err instanceof TypeError || err.retryable === true;
      if (!isNetworkError || attempt >= maxRetries) {
        throw err;
      }
      const delayMs = 300 * Math.pow(2, attempt) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);
      attempt++;
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Memoizes the in-flight/resolved JSON promise per URL (deletes on failure)
 * and checks signal?.throwIfAborted() before and after awaiting so aborting
 * one caller never cancels a shared network request.
 */
export async function getJSON(url, signal = null) {
  signal?.throwIfAborted?.();

  let promise = inFlightRequests.get(url);
  if (!promise) {
    promise = fetchJsonWithRetry(url, 3).catch((err) => {
      inFlightRequests.delete(url);
      throw err;
    });
    inFlightRequests.set(url, promise);
  }

  const data = await promise;
  signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
  return data;
}

/**
 * Normalizes a string for accent-insensitive, punctuation-insensitive search.
 * Converts ♀ -> "f" and ♂ -> "m" before stripping non-alphanumerics so
 * "nidoran♀", "farfetch'd", "mr. mime", and "flabébé" match cleanly.
 */
export function normalizeSearchString(str) {
  if (!str) return "";
  return String(str)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/♀/g, "f")
    .replace(/♂/g, "m")
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
}

/**
 * Maps raw.githubusercontent.com sprite URLs to the jsDelivr CDN mirror
 * to prevent connection timeouts on restricted networks.
 */
export function fixSpriteUrl(url) {
  if (!url || typeof url !== "string") return url;
  return url.replace(
    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/",
    "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/"
  );
}

export const MAX_POKEMON_ID = 1025;

  2: { start: 152, end: 251 },

  4: { start: 387, end: 493 },

  7: { start: 722, end: 809 },

  9: { start: 906, end: MAX_POKEMON_ID },
};

const TYPE_CHART = {
  normal: { rock: 0.5, ghost: 0, steel: 0.5 },
  fire: { fire: 0.5, water: 0.5, grass: 2, ice: 2, bug: 2, rock: 0.5, dragon: 0.5, steel: 2 },
  water: { fire: 2, water: 0.5, grass: 0.5, ground: 2, rock: 2, dragon: 0.5 },
  grass: { fire: 0.5, water: 2, grass: 0.5, poison: 0.5, ground: 2, flying: 0.5, bug: 0.5, rock: 2, dragon: 0.5, steel: 0.5 },
  electric: { water: 2, grass: 0.5, electric: 0.5, ground: 0, flying: 2, dragon: 0.5 },
  ice: { fire: 0.5, water: 0.5, grass: 2, ice: 0.5, ground: 2, flying: 2, dragon: 2, steel: 0.5 },
  fighting: { normal: 2, ice: 2, poison: 0.5, flying: 0.5, psychic: 0.5, bug: 0.5, rock: 2, ghost: 0, dark: 2, steel: 2, fairy: 0.5 },
  poison: { grass: 2, poison: 0.5, ground: 0.5, rock: 0.5, ghost: 0.5, steel: 0, fairy: 2 },
  ground: { fire: 2, grass: 0.5, electric: 2, poison: 2, flying: 0, bug: 0.5, rock: 2, steel: 2 },
  flying: { grass: 2, electric: 0.5, fighting: 2, bug: 2, rock: 0.5, steel: 0.5 },
  psychic: { fighting: 2, poison: 2, psychic: 0.5, dark: 0, steel: 0.5 },
  bug: { fire: 0.5, grass: 2, fighting: 0.5, poison: 0.5, flying: 0.5, psychic: 2, ghost: 0.5, dark: 2, steel: 0.5, fairy: 0.5 },
  rock: { fire: 2, ice: 2, fighting: 0.5, ground: 0.5, flying: 2, bug: 2, steel: 0.5 },
  ghost: { normal: 0, psychic: 2, ghost: 2, dark: 0.5 },
  dragon: { dragon: 2, steel: 0.5, fairy: 0 },
  steel: { fire: 0.5, water: 0.5, electric: 0.5, ice: 2, rock: 2, steel: 0.5, fairy: 2 },
  fairy: { fire: 0.5, fighting: 2, poison: 0.5, dragon: 2, dark: 2, steel: 0.5 },
  dark: { fighting: 0.5, psychic: 2, ghost: 2, dark: 0.5, fairy: 0.5 }
};

export function getTypeMatchups(pokemonTypes) {
  const ALL_TYPES = Object.keys(TYPE_CHART);
  const weaknesses = [];
  const resistances = [];

  ALL_TYPES.forEach((attacker) => {
    let multiplier = 1;
    pokemonTypes.forEach((defType) => {
      const lowerDef = defType.toLowerCase();
      if (TYPE_CHART[attacker] && TYPE_CHART[attacker][lowerDef] !== undefined) {
        multiplier *= TYPE_CHART[attacker][lowerDef];
      }
    });

    if (multiplier > 1) {
      weaknesses.push({ type: attacker, multiplier });
    } else if (multiplier < 1) {
      resistances.push({ type: attacker, multiplier });
    }
  });

  return { weaknesses, resistances };
}

export function formatPokemonName(name, speciesName = "", canonicalBaseName = "") {
  if (!name) return "";

  const capitalize = (str) =>
    str
      .split("-")
      .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
      .join(" ");

  const lowerName = name.toLowerCase();
  const baseSlug = speciesName
    ? speciesName.toLowerCase()
    : inferBaseSpeciesSlug(lowerName);
  const displayBase = canonicalBaseName || capitalize(baseSlug);

  if (lowerName === baseSlug && canonicalBaseName) {
    return canonicalBaseName;
  }

  // Paldean Tauros Breeds
  if (lowerName === "tauros-paldea-combat-breed") return `Paldean ${displayBase} (Combat Breed)`;
  if (lowerName === "tauros-paldea-blaze-breed") return `Paldean ${displayBase} (Blaze Breed)`;
  if (lowerName === "tauros-paldea-aqua-breed") return `Paldean ${displayBase} (Aqua Breed)`;

  // Specific Regional & Special Edge Cases
  if (lowerName === "farfetchd-galar") return "Galarian Farfetch'd";
  if (lowerName === "mr-mime-galar") return "Galarian Mr. Mime";
  if (lowerName === "darmanitan-galar-zen") return `Galarian ${displayBase} (Zen Mode)`;
  if (lowerName === "greninja-ash") return "Ash-Greninja";
  if (lowerName === "basculin-white-striped") return `White-Striped ${displayBase}`;
  if (lowerName === "ursaluna-bloodmoon") return `Bloodmoon ${displayBase}`;

  // Standard Regional Forms
  if (lowerName.endsWith("-alola")) return `Alolan ${displayBase}`;
  if (lowerName.endsWith("-galar")) return `Galarian ${displayBase}`;
  if (lowerName.endsWith("-paldea")) return `Paldean ${displayBase}`;
  if (lowerName.endsWith("-hisui")) return `Hisuian ${displayBase}`;

  // Battle Gimmicks & Megas
  if (lowerName.includes("-mega")) {
    const suffix = lowerName.split("-mega")[1]?.replace(/^-/, "");
    const suffixFormatted = suffix ? ` ${capitalize(suffix)}` : "";
    return `Mega ${displayBase}${suffixFormatted}`;
  }
  if (lowerName.includes("-gmax")) return `Gigantamax ${displayBase}`;
  if (lowerName.includes("-primal")) return `Primal ${displayBase}`;

  // Species fallback for form suffixes
  if (baseSlug && lowerName.startsWith(baseSlug + "-")) {
    const formSuffix = lowerName.slice(baseSlug.length + 1);
    return `${displayBase} (${capitalize(formSuffix)})`;
  }

  return canonicalBaseName || capitalize(name);
}

export function calculateStatBounds(statName, base) {
  if (statName === "hp") {
    if (base === 1) return { min: 1, max: 1 };
    const min = Math.floor(((2 * base + 0 + 0) * 100) / 100) + 100 + 10;
    const max = Math.floor(((2 * base + 31 + 63) * 100) / 100) + 100 + 10;
    return { min, max };
  }
  const min = Math.floor((Math.floor(((2 * base + 0 + 0) * 100) / 100) + 5) * 0.9);
  const max = Math.floor((Math.floor(((2 * base + 31 + 63) * 100) / 100) + 5) * 1.1);
  return { min, max };
}

export function parseEvolutionDetails(detailsList) {
  if (!detailsList || detailsList.length === 0) return "";

  const uniqueConditions = new Set();

  detailsList.forEach((d) => {
    const parts = [];
    const triggerName = d.trigger?.name || "";

    if (triggerName === "level-up") {
      if (d.min_level) parts.push(`Level ${d.min_level}`);
      if (d.min_happiness) parts.push("High Friendship");
      if (d.min_affection) parts.push(`Affection ${d.min_affection}+`);
      if (d.known_move) parts.push(`Knows ${formatPokemonName(d.known_move.name)}`);
      if (d.known_move_type) parts.push(`Knows ${formatPokemonName(d.known_move_type.name)}-type move`);
      if (d.held_item) parts.push(`Hold ${formatPokemonName(d.held_item.name)}`);
      if (d.time_of_day) parts.push(`(${d.time_of_day})`);
      if (d.location) parts.push(`at ${formatPokemonName(d.location.name)}`);
      if (parts.length === 0) parts.push("Level up");
    } else if (triggerName === "use-item") {
      parts.push(d.item ? `Use ${formatPokemonName(d.item.name)}` : "Use item");
      if (d.time_of_day) parts.push(`(${d.time_of_day})`);
    } else if (triggerName === "trade") {
      if (d.trade_species) {
        parts.push(`Trade for ${formatPokemonName(d.trade_species.name)}`);
      } else if (d.held_item) {
        parts.push(`Trade holding ${formatPokemonName(d.held_item.name)}`);
      } else {
        parts.push("Trade");
      }
    } else if (triggerName === "shed") {
      parts.push("Open slot & Poké Ball");
    } else if (triggerName) {
      parts.push(formatPokemonName(triggerName));
    }

    // Shared modifier conditions applicable across triggers
    if (d.gender === 1) parts.push("(Female)");
    else if (d.gender === 2) parts.push("(Male)");

    if (d.party_species) {
      parts.push(`with ${formatPokemonName(d.party_species.name)} in party`);
    }
    if (d.party_type) {
      parts.push(`with ${formatPokemonName(d.party_type.name)}-type in party`);
    }
    if (d.relative_physical_stats === 1) {
      parts.push("(Atk > Def)");
    } else if (d.relative_physical_stats === -1) {
      parts.push("(Atk < Def)");
    } else if (d.relative_physical_stats === 0) {
      parts.push("(Atk = Def)");
    }
    if (d.needs_overworld_rain) {
      parts.push("in Rain");
    }
    if (d.turn_upside_down) {
      parts.push("(Hold console upside down)");
    }

    const text = parts.join(" ").trim();
    if (text) uniqueConditions.add(text);
  });

  return Array.from(uniqueConditions).join(" / ");
}

function parseEvolutionChain(chainNode) {
  const result = {
    speciesName: chainNode.species.name,
    speciesUrl: chainNode.species.url,
    evolutionRequirement: parseEvolutionDetails(chainNode.evolution_details),
    evolvesTo: [],
    battleForms: []
  };

  if (chainNode.evolves_to && chainNode.evolves_to.length > 0) {
    result.evolvesTo = chainNode.evolves_to.map(parseEvolutionChain);
  }

  return result;
}

function buildLinearChainNode(steps, index = 0) {
  if (index >= steps.length) return null;
  const current = steps[index];
  const node = {
    speciesName: current.name,
    displayName: formatPokemonName(current.name),
    speciesUrl: String(current.id),
    customImage: `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${current.id}.png`,
    evolutionRequirement: current.req || "",
    evolvesTo: [],
    battleForms: BATTLE_FORMS_REGISTRY[current.name.toLowerCase()] || []
  };
  const next = buildLinearChainNode(steps, index + 1);
  if (next) node.evolvesTo = [next];
  return node;
}

export function buildCompleteEvolutionTree(rawName, baseParsedChain) {
  const lowerName = rawName.toLowerCase();

  // 1. Isolated dedicated regional line check
  if (ISOLATED_REGIONAL_TREES[lowerName]) {
    return buildLinearChainNode(ISOLATED_REGIONAL_TREES[lowerName]);
  }

  // 2. Standard chain traversal with parallel sibling injections and battleForms attachment
  function processChainNode(node) {
    if (!node) return null;
    const cleanKey = node.speciesName.toLowerCase();

    // Attach battle forms (Megas, Primals, Ash-Greninja)
    node.battleForms = BATTLE_FORMS_REGISTRY[cleanKey] || [];

    // Inject regional parallel branches as siblings
    const extraSiblings = SIBLING_EVO_INJECTIONS[cleanKey] || [];
    if (extraSiblings.length > 0) {
      extraSiblings.forEach((sibling) => {
        const alreadyExists = node.evolvesTo.some(
          (c) => c.speciesName.toLowerCase() === sibling.speciesName.toLowerCase()
        );
        if (!alreadyExists) {
          node.evolvesTo.push({
            speciesName: sibling.speciesName,
            displayName: sibling.displayName,
            speciesUrl: String(sibling.id),
            customImage: `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${sibling.id}.png`,
            evolutionRequirement: sibling.requirement,
            evolvesTo: [],
            battleForms: BATTLE_FORMS_REGISTRY[sibling.speciesName.toLowerCase()] || []
          });
        }
      });
    }

    if (node.evolvesTo && node.evolvesTo.length > 0) {
      node.evolvesTo.forEach(processChainNode);
    }

    return node;
  }

  return processChainNode(baseParsedChain);
}

function getEnglishSpeciesName(speciesData) {
  if (!speciesData?.names) return "";
  const enEntry = speciesData.names.find((n) => n.language?.name === "en");
  return enEntry ? enEntry.name : "";
}

function getFriendshipLabel(val) {
  if (val === 0) return "lowest";
  if (val < 50) return "low";
  if (val <= 70) return "normal";
  if (val < 100) return "high";
  return "very high";
}

export function normalizePokemonSummary(rawData, speciesData = null) {
  const animatedImage =
    fixSpriteUrl(
      rawData.sprites?.other?.showdown?.front_default ||
      rawData.sprites?.versions?.["generation-v"]?.["black-white"]?.animated?.front_default
    );

  const officialArtwork =
    rawData.sprites?.other?.["official-artwork"]?.front_default ||
    rawData.sprites?.front_default;

  const shinyArtwork =
    rawData.sprites?.other?.["official-artwork"]?.front_shiny ||
    rawData.sprites?.front_shiny ||
    `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/shiny/${rawData.id}.png`;

  const pixelSprite =
    rawData.sprites?.front_default ||
    `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/${rawData.id}.png`;

  let description = "No Pokédex description available.";
  let genus = "";
  let generation = null;

  if (speciesData?.generation?.url) {
    const genParts = speciesData.generation.url.split("/").filter(Boolean);
    const parsedGen = parseInt(genParts[genParts.length - 1], 10);
    if (!isNaN(parsedGen)) generation = parsedGen;
  }

  if (speciesData) {
    if (speciesData.flavor_text_entries) {
      const englishEntries = speciesData.flavor_text_entries.filter(
        (entry) => entry.language?.name === "en"
      );
      if (englishEntries.length > 0) {
        const lastEntry = englishEntries[englishEntries.length - 1];
        description = lastEntry.flavor_text
          .replace(/[\f\n\r]/g, " ")
          .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
          .trim();
      }
    }

    if (speciesData.genera) {
      const englishGenus = speciesData.genera.find((g) => g.language?.name === "en");
      if (englishGenus) genus = englishGenus.genus;
    }
  }

  const speciesName = speciesData?.name || rawData.species?.name || "";
  const canonicalBaseName = getEnglishSpeciesName(speciesData);

  const stats = {
    hp: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "hp")?.base_stat || 0,
    attack: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "attack")?.base_stat || 0,
    defense: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "defense")?.base_stat || 0,
    spAtk: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "special-attack")?.base_stat || 0,
    spDef: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "special-defense")?.base_stat || 0,
    speed: rawData.stats?.find((s) => s.stat.name === "speed")?.base_stat || 0,
  };

  const abilities = (rawData.abilities || []).map((a) => ({
    name: formatPokemonName(a.ability.name),
    rawName: a.ability.name,
    isHidden: a.is_hidden,
    slot: a.slot,
    url: a.ability.url,
  }));

  return {
    id: rawData.id,
    name: formatPokemonName(rawData.name, speciesName, canonicalBaseName),
    rawName: rawData.name,
    speciesName: speciesName || rawData.name,
    generation,
    genus: genus || "Pokémon",
    height: rawData.height ? (rawData.height / 10).toFixed(1) + " m" : "N/A",
    weight: rawData.weight ? (rawData.weight / 10).toFixed(1) + " kg" : "N/A",
    animatedImage: animatedImage || officialArtwork,
    officialArtwork,
    shinyArtwork,
    sprite: pixelSprite,
    image: animatedImage || officialArtwork,
    types: rawData.types?.map((t) => t.type.name) || [],
    stats,
    abilities,
    cry: rawData.cries?.latest || rawData.cries?.legacy || null,
    description,
    isDetailed: false,
  };
}

export function normalizePokemonData(rawData, speciesData = null, evolutionData = null) {
  const summary = normalizePokemonSummary(rawData, speciesData);
  const canonicalBaseName = getEnglishSpeciesName(speciesData);

  const flavorTextEntries = [];
  if (speciesData?.flavor_text_entries) {
    speciesData.flavor_text_entries.forEach((entry) => {
      if (entry.language?.name === "en") {
        const cleanText = entry.flavor_text
          .replace(/[\f\n\r]/g, " ")
          .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
          .trim();
        flavorTextEntries.push({
          version: entry.version.name,
          flavorText: cleanText,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  const statBounds = {
    hp: calculateStatBounds("hp", summary.stats.hp),
    attack: calculateStatBounds("attack", summary.stats.attack),
    defense: calculateStatBounds("defense", summary.stats.defense),
    spAtk: calculateStatBounds("special-attack", summary.stats.spAtk),
    spDef: calculateStatBounds("special-defense", summary.stats.spDef),
    speed: calculateStatBounds("speed", summary.stats.speed),
  };

  const evYieldList = [];
  rawData.stats?.forEach((s) => {
    if (s.effort > 0) {
      evYieldList.push(`${s.effort} ${formatPokemonName(s.stat.name)}`);
    }
  });

  const heldItems = (rawData.held_items || []).map((h) => ({
    name: formatPokemonName(h.item.name),
    rarity: h.version_details?.[0]?.rarity ?? null,
  }));

  const catchRate = speciesData?.capture_rate ?? null;
  const baseFriendship = speciesData?.base_happiness ?? null;
  const baseExp = rawData.base_experience ?? null;
  const growthRate = speciesData?.growth_rate?.name
    ? formatPokemonName(speciesData.growth_rate.name)
    : "Medium";
  const eggGroups =
    speciesData?.egg_groups?.map((g) => formatPokemonName(g.name)) || ["Undiscovered"];
  const genderRate = speciesData?.gender_rate ?? -1;
  const hatchCounter = speciesData?.hatch_counter ?? null;

  const varieties = (speciesData?.varieties || []).map((v) => {
    const vUrlParts = v.pokemon.url.split("/").filter(Boolean);
    const formId = vUrlParts[vUrlParts.length - 1];
    return {
      name: formatPokemonName(v.pokemon.name, summary.speciesName, canonicalBaseName),
      rawName: v.pokemon.name,
      id: formId,
      image: `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${formId}.png`,
      isDefault: v.is_default,
    };
  });

  const moves = (rawData.moves || []).map((m) => {
    const versions = m.version_group_details.map((vg) => ({
      versionGroup: vg.version_group.name,
      learnMethod: vg.move_learn_method.name,
      levelLearned: vg.level_learned_at,
    }));

    return {
      name: formatPokemonName(m.move.name),
      rawName: m.move.name,
      versions,
    };
  });

  const parsedBaseTree = evolutionData ? parseEvolutionChain(evolutionData.chain) : null;
  const finalEvolutionTree = buildCompleteEvolutionTree(rawData.name, parsedBaseTree);

  const catchPct =
    catchRate !== null ? Math.round(((catchRate / 765) * 100) * 10) / 10 : null;

  return {
    ...summary,
    statBounds,
    flavorTextEntries,
    evYield: evYieldList.length > 0 ? evYieldList.join(", ") : "None",
    catchRate:
      catchRate !== null
        ? `${catchRate} (~${catchPct}% with Poké Ball at full HP)`
        : "N/A",
    baseFriendship:
      baseFriendship !== null
        ? `${baseFriendship} (${getFriendshipLabel(baseFriendship)})`
        : "N/A",
    baseExp: baseExp !== null ? `${baseExp}` : "N/A",
    growthRate,
    heldItems,
    eggGroups,
    genderRate,
    hatchCounter:
      hatchCounter !== null
        ? `${hatchCounter * 255} steps (${hatchCounter} cycles)`
        : "N/A",
    varieties,
    moves,
    evolutionTree: finalEvolutionTree,
    isDetailed: true,
  };
}

/**
 * Lightweight fetch for the mini-game (fetches only /pokemon/{id} if not already cached).
 */
export async function fetchPokemonMinigame(nameOrId, signal = null) {
  const cached = getCachedPokemon(nameOrId);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const cleanQuery = encodeURIComponent(
    String(nameOrId).toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, "-")
  );
  const rawData = await getJSON(`${BASE_URL}/${cleanQuery}`, signal);
  const animatedImage =
    fixSpriteUrl(
      rawData.sprites?.other?.showdown?.front_default ||
      rawData.sprites?.versions?.["generation-v"]?.["black-white"]?.animated?.front_default
    );
  const officialArtwork =
    rawData.sprites?.other?.["official-artwork"]?.front_default ||
    rawData.sprites?.front_default;

  return {
    id: rawData.id,
    name: formatPokemonName(rawData.name, rawData.species?.name || ""),
    rawName: rawData.name,
    officialArtwork: officialArtwork || animatedImage,
    image: officialArtwork || animatedImage,
    cry: rawData.cries?.latest || rawData.cries?.legacy || null,
  };
}

/**
 * Fetches summary data (pokemon + species) for grid, team, favorites, PC box, and scanner.
 */
export async function fetchPokemonSummary(nameOrId, signal = null) {
  const cached = getCachedPokemon(nameOrId);
  if (cached) {
    signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
    return cached;
  }

  try {
    const cleanQuery = encodeURIComponent(
      String(nameOrId).toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, "-")
    );
    const rawData = await getJSON(`${BASE_URL}/${cleanQuery}`, signal);
    let speciesData = null;

    try {
      const speciesIdentifier = encodeURIComponent(rawData.species?.name || rawData.id);
      speciesData = await getJSON(`${SPECIES_URL}/${speciesIdentifier}`, signal);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.name === "AbortError") throw e;
      console.warn("Could not fetch species data:", e);
    }

    const summary = normalizePokemonSummary(rawData, speciesData);
    setCachedPokemon(summary);
    return summary;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError") throw error;
    console.error("API Summary Error:", error.message);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Fetches complete detail data (including evolution chain, moves, varieties, training/breeding)
 * when the detail modal opens.
 */
export async function fetchPokemonDetail(nameOrId, signal = null) {
  const cached = getCachedPokemon(nameOrId);
  if (cached && cached.isDetailed) {
    signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
    return cached;
  }

  try {
    const cleanQuery = encodeURIComponent(
      String(nameOrId).toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, "-")
    );
    const rawData = await getJSON(`${BASE_URL}/${cleanQuery}`, signal);
    let speciesData = null;
    let evolutionData = null;

    try {
      const speciesIdentifier = encodeURIComponent(rawData.species?.name || rawData.id);
      speciesData = await getJSON(`${SPECIES_URL}/${speciesIdentifier}`, signal);
      if (speciesData?.evolution_chain?.url) {
        evolutionData = await getJSON(speciesData.evolution_chain.url, signal);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.name === "AbortError") throw e;
      console.warn("Could not fetch species/evolution data:", e);
    }

    const detailed = normalizePokemonData(rawData, speciesData, evolutionData);
    setCachedPokemon(detailed);
    return detailed;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError") throw error;
    console.error("API Detail Error:", error.message);
    throw error;
  }
}

// Alias kept for backward compatibility if any external caller imports fetchPokemon
export const fetchPokemon = fetchPokemonDetail;

let secondaryTypeIdsCache = null;

export async function getPokemonListByType(typeName, slot = null, excludeForms = true, signal = null) {
  const safeType = encodeURIComponent(String(typeName).toLowerCase().trim());
  const data = await getJSON(`${API}/type/${safeType}`, signal);
  let entries = data.pokemon;

  if (slot !== null) {
    entries = entries.filter((entry) => entry.slot === slot);
  }

  const results = entries.map((entry) => {
    const urlParts = entry.pokemon.url.split("/").filter(Boolean);
    const id = parseInt(urlParts[urlParts.length - 1], 10);
    return { name: entry.pokemon.name, id, slot: entry.slot };
  });

  if (excludeForms) {
    return results.filter((p) => p.id < 10000);
  }
  return results;
}

export async function getAllSecondaryTypeIds(signal = null) {
  if (secondaryTypeIdsCache) return secondaryTypeIdsCache;

  const ALL_TYPES = Object.keys(TYPE_CHART);
  const requests = ALL_TYPES.map(async (type) => {
    try {
      const list = await getPokemonListByType(type, 2, true, signal);
      return list.map((p) => p.id);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name === "AbortError") throw err;
      return [];
    }
  });

  const idArrays = await Promise.all(requests);
  secondaryTypeIdsCache = new Set(idArrays.flat());
  return secondaryTypeIdsCache;
}

/**
 * Fetches a slice of Pokémon summaries with concurrency limited to 8 workers,
 * automatic retry with exponential backoff (via getJSON), and returns a `failed` list.
 */
export async function fetchPokemonBatch(
  items,
  startIndex,
  batchSize = 30,
  signal = null,
  concurrency = 8
) {
  const slice = items.slice(startIndex, startIndex + batchSize);
  const results = new Array(slice.length).fill(null);
  const failed = [];
  let cursor = 0;

  async function worker() {
    while (cursor < slice.length) {
      signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
      const idx = cursor++;
      const item = slice[idx];
      const identifier = typeof item === "object" ? item.name || item.id : item;

      try {
        const mon = await fetchPokemonSummary(identifier, signal);
        results[idx] = mon;
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.name === "AbortError" || signal?.aborted) throw err;
        console.warn(`Failed to load Pokémon ${identifier}:`, err);
        failed.push(identifier);
      }
    }
  }

  const workerCount = Math.min(concurrency, slice.length);
  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()));

  const validPokemon = results.filter(Boolean);
  const nextIndex = startIndex + batchSize;

  return {
    pokemonList: validPokemon,
    failed,
    nextIndex,
    hasMore: nextIndex < items.length,
  };
}

export function getFormOrSpeciesGen(pokemon) {
  // 1. Prefer explicit species generation if available on normalized data
  if (typeof pokemon === "object" && pokemon !== null && pokemon.generation) {
    return Number(pokemon.generation);
  }

  const name = (typeof pokemon === "object" ? pokemon.rawName || pokemon.name : String(pokemon)).toLowerCase();

  // 2. Name-based form heuristics
  if (name.includes("-alola")) return 7;
  if (name.includes("-galar") || name.includes("-hisui")) return 8;
  if (name.includes("-paldea")) return 9;
  if (name.includes("-mega") || name.includes("-primal")) return 6;
  if (name.includes("-gmax")) return 8;

  // 3. Fallback by National Dex ID range (numeric gens 1..9 only)
  const id = typeof pokemon === "object" ? Number(pokemon.id) : Number(pokemon);
  if (!isNaN(id)) {
    for (let gen = 1; gen <= 9; gen++) {
      const range = GEN_RANGES[gen];
      if (range && id >= range.start && id <= range.end) {
        return gen;
      }
    }
  }

  return 1;
}

export function isItemInGenRange(item, targetGen) {
  if (targetGen === "all") return true;
  const numGen = Number(targetGen);
  return getFormOrSpeciesGen(item) === numGen;
}

export function inferBaseSpeciesSlug(rawName, knownBaseSpeciesSet = HYPHENATED_BASE_SPECIES) {
  const lower = String(rawName || "").toLowerCase();
  if (knownBaseSpeciesSet.has(lower)) return lower;

  for (const hyphenBase of HYPHENATED_BASE_SPECIES) {
    if (lower === hyphenBase || lower.startsWith(hyphenBase + "-")) {
      return hyphenBase;
    }
  }

  const parts = lower.split("-");
  if (parts.length >= 2) {
    const twoPart = `${parts[0]}-${parts[1]}`;
    if (knownBaseSpeciesSet.has(twoPart)) return twoPart;
  }

  return parts[0];
}

export function deriveFormTagsFromName(rawName, baseSpecies = "") {
  const lower = String(rawName || "").toLowerCase();
  const tags = new Set();

  if (lower.includes("-alola")) {
    tags.add("alola");
    tags.add("alolan");
    tags.add("regional");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-galar")) {
    tags.add("galar");
    tags.add("galarian");
    tags.add("regional");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-hisui")) {
    tags.add("hisui");
    tags.add("hisuian");
    tags.add("regional");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-paldea")) {
    tags.add("paldea");
    tags.add("paldean");
    tags.add("regional");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-mega")) {
    tags.add("mega");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-gmax")) {
    tags.add("gmax");
    tags.add("dynamax");
    tags.add("gigantamax");
  }
  if (lower.includes("-primal")) {
    tags.add("primal");
  }
  if (lower === "greninja-ash") {
    tags.add("battle-bond");
    tags.add("ash-greninja");
    tags.add("special");
  }

  if (baseSpecies && lower.startsWith(baseSpecies + "-")) {
    const suffixParts = lower.slice(baseSpecies.length + 1).split("-");
    suffixParts.forEach((p) => {
      if (p) tags.add(p);
    });
  }

  return Array.from(tags);
}

export async function fetchFullPokedexDirectory(signal = null) {
  // 1. Check versioned localStorage cache with TTL
  try {
    const rawCache = localStorage.getItem(DIRECTORY_CACHE_KEY);
    if (rawCache) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(rawCache);
      if (
        parsed &&
        parsed.timestamp &&
        Date.now() - parsed.timestamp < DIRECTORY_CACHE_TTL &&
        Array.isArray(parsed.data) &&
        parsed.data.length > 0
      ) {
        return parsed.data;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Could not read directory cache from localStorage:", e);
  }

  try {
    const data = await getJSON(`${BASE_URL}?limit=100000`, signal);

    const specialFormsMap = new Map(
      SPECIAL_FORM_REGISTRY.map((item) => [item.name.toLowerCase(), item])
    );

    const baseSpeciesSet = new Set(HYPHENATED_BASE_SPECIES);
    for (const p of data.results || []) {
      const urlParts = p.url.split("/").filter(Boolean);
      const pokeId = parseInt(urlParts[urlParts.length - 1], 10);
      if (pokeId >= 1 && pokeId <= MAX_POKEMON_ID) {
        baseSpeciesSet.add(p.name.toLowerCase());
      }
    }

    const directory = [];
    const seenIds = new Set();
    const seenNames = new Set();

    // Ingest official PokéAPI entries only (no fake 10000+ or 20000+ IDs)
    for (const p of data.results || []) {
      const lowerName = p.name.toLowerCase();
      const urlParts = p.url.split("/").filter(Boolean);
      const pokeId = parseInt(urlParts[urlParts.length - 1], 10);

      if (isNaN(pokeId) || seenIds.has(pokeId) || seenNames.has(lowerName)) continue;
      seenIds.add(pokeId);
      seenNames.add(lowerName);

      const specialMeta = specialFormsMap.get(lowerName);
      const baseSpecies = specialMeta
        ? specialMeta.base.toLowerCase()
        : inferBaseSpeciesSlug(lowerName, baseSpeciesSet);

      const derivedTags = deriveFormTagsFromName(lowerName, baseSpecies);
      const combinedTags = specialMeta
        ? Array.from(new Set([...specialMeta.tags, ...derivedTags]))
        : derivedTags;

      directory.push({
        id: pokeId,
        name: p.name,
        baseSpecies,
        displayName: formatPokemonName(p.name, baseSpecies),
        forms: combinedTags,
      });
    }

    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        DIRECTORY_CACHE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({ timestamp: Date.now(), data: directory })
      );
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Could not write directory cache to localStorage:", e);
    }

    return directory;
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === "AbortError") throw err;
    console.error("Failed to load directory:", err);
    return [];
  }
}
