    value: "black-white",
    label: "Black / White (Gen 5)",
    moveGroups: ["black-white", "black-2-white-2"],
    flavorVersions: ["black", "white", "black-2", "white-2"],
  },
  {
    value: "diamond-pearl",
    label: "Diamond / Pearl (Gen 4)",
    moveGroups: ["diamond-pearl", "platinum", "heartgold-soulsilver"],
    flavorVersions: ["diamond", "pearl", "platinum", "heartgold", "soulsilver"],
  },
  {
    value: "ruby-sapphire",
    label: "Ruby / Sapphire (Gen 3)",
    moveGroups: ["ruby-sapphire", "emerald", "firered-leafgreen", "colosseum", "xd"],
    flavorVersions: ["ruby", "sapphire", "emerald", "firered", "leafgreen"],
  },
  {
    value: "gold-silver",
    label: "Gold / Silver / Crystal (Gen 2)",
    moveGroups: ["gold-silver", "crystal"],
    flavorVersions: ["gold", "silver", "crystal"],
  },
  {
    value: "red-blue",
    label: "Red / Blue / Yellow (Gen 1)",
    moveGroups: ["red-blue", "yellow"],
    flavorVersions: ["red", "blue", "yellow"],
  },
];

function getTeamSynergy(teamList) {
  const weaknessCounts = {};
  const resistanceCounts = {};

  teamList.forEach((pokemon) => {
    const { weaknesses, resistances } = getTypeMatchups(pokemon.types);
    weaknesses.forEach((w) => {
      weaknessCounts[w.type] = (weaknessCounts[w.type] || 0) + 1;
    });
    resistances.forEach((r) => {
      resistanceCounts[r.type] = (resistanceCounts[r.type] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return { weaknessCounts, resistanceCounts };
}

export function createPokemonCard(pokemon, spriteMode = "artwork") {
  const formattedId = `#${String(pokemon.id).padStart(3, "0")}`;
  const primaryType = pokemon.types[0] || "normal";

  const typeBadges = pokemon.types
    .map((type) => `<span class="type-badge type-${type}">${type}</span>`)
    .join("");

  // Determine correct image URL across the 3 modes ("artwork", "animated", "pixel")
  let displayImage = pokemon.officialArtwork || pokemon.image;
  if (spriteMode === "animated") {
    displayImage = pokemon.animatedImage || pokemon.officialArtwork || pokemon.image;
  } else if (spriteMode === "pixel") {
    displayImage = pokemon.sprite || `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/${pokemon.id}.png`;
  }
  displayImage = fixSpriteUrl(displayImage);

  const abilitiesText = (pokemon.abilities || [])
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((a) => a.name)
    .join(", ") || "None";

  const isCaught = isPokemonCaught(pokemon.id);
  const isFav = isPokemonFavorite(pokemon.id);

  return `
    <article class="pokemon-card type-glow-${primaryType}" data-id="${pokemon.id}">
      <div class="card-top-bar">
        <span class="card-id">${formattedId}</span>
        <div class="card-top-actions">
          <button class="icon-toggle-btn caught-btn ${isCaught ? 'active' : ''}" data-id="${pokemon.id}" title="${isCaught ? 'Identified / Caught' : 'Mark Caught'}" type="button">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>
          </button>
          <button class="icon-toggle-btn fav-btn ${isFav ? 'active' : ''}" data-id="${pokemon.id}" title="${isFav ? 'Favorited' : 'Favorite'}" type="button">
            <i class="${isFav ? 'fa-solid' : 'fa-regular'} fa-heart"></i>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card-media">
        <img src="${displayImage}" alt="${pokemon.name}" class="card-image" loading="lazy" />
      </div>

      <h3 class="pokemon-name">${pokemon.name}</h3>
      <p class="pokemon-genus">${pokemon.genus}</p>
      
      <div class="card-types">
        ${typeBadges}
      </div>

      <div class="card-metrics-row">
        <span><i class="fa-solid fa-ruler-vertical"></i> ${pokemon.height}</span>
        <span><i class="fa-solid fa-weight-hanging"></i> ${pokemon.weight}</span>
      </div>

      <div class="card-ability-pill">
        <span class="pill-label">Ability:</span> ${abilitiesText}
      </div>

      <p class="pokemon-description">"${pokemon.description}"</p>

      <div class="card-stats-preview">
        <div class="stat-mini">
          <span>HP</span>
          <div class="mini-bar-track"><div class="mini-bar-fill hp" style="width: ${Math.min(100, (pokemon.stats.hp / 255) * 100)}%"></div></div>
          <strong>${pokemon.stats.hp}</strong>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-mini">
          <span>ATK</span>
          <div class="mini-bar-track"><div class="mini-bar-fill atk" style="width: ${Math.min(100, (pokemon.stats.attack / 255) * 100)}%"></div></div>
          <strong>${pokemon.stats.attack}</strong>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-mini">
          <span>DEF</span>
          <div class="mini-bar-track"><div class="mini-bar-fill def" style="width: ${Math.min(100, (pokemon.stats.defense / 255) * 100)}%"></div></div>
          <strong>${pokemon.stats.defense}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card-actions">
        <button class="add-team-btn" data-id="${pokemon.id}">+ Add to Team</button>
      </div>
    </article>
  `;
}

export function renderPokemonGrid(pokemonList, containerElement, spriteMode = "artwork", append = false) {
  if (!append) {
    containerElement.innerHTML = "";
  }

  if (!pokemonList || pokemonList.length === 0) {
    if (!append) {
      containerElement.innerHTML = `<p class="status-message">No Pokémon found.</p>`;
    }
    return;
  }

  const cardsHtml = pokemonList
    .map((pokemon) => createPokemonCard(pokemon, spriteMode))
    .join("");

  if (append) {
    const scrollLoader = document.getElementById("scroll-loader");
    if (scrollLoader) scrollLoader.remove();

    // Insert before sentinel so the sentinel stays at the very end of the grid
    const sentinel = containerElement.querySelector("#grid-sentinel");
    if (sentinel) {
      sentinel.insertAdjacentHTML("beforebegin", cardsHtml);
    } else {
      containerElement.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", cardsHtml);
    }
  } else {
    containerElement.innerHTML = cardsHtml;
  }
}

export function renderFavoritesGrid(pokemonList, containerElement) {
  if (!pokemonList || pokemonList.length === 0) {
    containerElement.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-empty-state">No favorite Pokémon recorded yet.</div>`;
    return;
  }

  containerElement.innerHTML = pokemonList
    .map((p) => {
      const typeBadges = (p.types || [])
        .map((t) => `<span class="type-badge type-${t}">${t}</span>`)
        .join("");
      const officialArt = p.officialArtwork || p.image;

      return `
        <div class="fav-compact-card">
          <button type="button" class="fav-unfavorite-btn" data-id="${p.id}" title="Unfavorite">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>
          </button>
          <img src="${officialArt}" alt="${p.name}" class="fav-compact-img" loading="lazy" />
          <span class="fav-compact-id">#${String(p.id).padStart(3, "0")}</span>
          <h4 class="fav-compact-name">${p.name}</h4>
          <div class="fav-compact-types">${typeBadges}</div>
        </div>
      `;
    })
    .join("");
}

function getArtworkForEvoNode(evoNode) {
  if (evoNode.customImage) return evoNode.customImage;

  const idParts = (evoNode.speciesUrl || "").split("/").filter(Boolean);
  const numericId = idParts.length > 0 ? idParts[idParts.length - 1] : null;

  if (numericId && !isNaN(Number(numericId))) {
    return `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${numericId}.png`;
  }
  return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/gen5/${evoNode.speciesName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "")}.png`;
}

function renderBattleFormsRow(battleForms, currentRawName) {
  if (!battleForms || battleForms.length === 0) return "";

  const cardsHtml = battleForms
    .map((form) => {
      const isCurrent = form.name.toLowerCase() === currentRawName.toLowerCase();
      const primaryImg = getBattleFormImageSrc(form);
      const fallbackImg = getBaseArtworkFallback(form);

      return `
        <div class="battle-fork-item">
          <div class="battle-connector">
            <span class="battle-trigger-pill">${form.trigger}</span>
            <span class="evo-arrow-char">➜</span>
          </div>
          <div class="evo-node battle-form-card ${isCurrent ? "active" : ""}" data-species="${form.name}" title="${form.displayName}">
            <div class="battle-form-tag">⚡ Mega Form</div>
            <div class="evo-avatar-wrap">
              <img 
                src="${primaryImg}" 
                alt="${form.displayName}" 
                class="evo-img" 
                loading="lazy" 
                onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallbackImg}';"
              />
            </div>
            <span class="evo-name">${form.displayName}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    })
    .join("");

  return `
    <div class="battle-forms-fork-container">
      ${cardsHtml}
    </div>
  `;
}

function getBattleFormImageSrc(form) {
  if (form.image || form.customImage) {
    return form.image || form.customImage;
  }

  if (form.id && !isNaN(Number(form.id))) {
    return `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${form.id}.png`;
  }

  const cleanName = (form.name || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  return `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${cleanName}.png`;
}

function getBaseArtworkFallback(form) {
  const baseName = form.baseSpecies || form.name.split("-")[0];
  return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/dex/${baseName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "")}.png`;
}

export function buildEvolutionTreeHtml(evoNode, currentRawName, varieties = []) {
  if (!evoNode) return `<p class="empty-note">This Pokémon does not evolve.</p>`;

  if (evoNode.battleForms && Array.isArray(evoNode.battleForms)) {
    evoNode.battleForms.forEach((form) => {
      const match = (varieties || []).find(
        (v) => (v.rawName || v.name || "").toLowerCase() === (form.name || "").toLowerCase()
      );
      if (match && match.image) {
        form.image = match.image;
      }
    });
  }

  const isCurrent = evoNode.speciesName.toLowerCase() === currentRawName.toLowerCase();
  const artworkUrl = getArtworkForEvoNode(evoNode);
  const displayName = evoNode.displayName || formatPokemonName(evoNode.speciesName);
  const fallbackUrl = `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/dex/${evoNode.speciesName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "")}.png`;

  const nodeCard = `
    <div class="evo-node ${isCurrent ? "active" : ""}" data-species="${evoNode.speciesName}">
      <div class="evo-avatar-wrap">
        <img 
          src="${artworkUrl}" 
          alt="${displayName}" 
          class="evo-img" 
          loading="lazy" 
          onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallbackUrl}';"
        />
      </div>
      <span class="evo-name">${displayName}</span>
    </div>
  `;

  const battleForms = evoNode.battleForms || [];
  let evolvesTo = [...(evoNode.evolvesTo || [])];

  if (evoNode.speciesName.toLowerCase() === "sandshrew" && evolvesTo.length === 1) {
    const origReq = evolvesTo[0].evolutionRequirement || "";
    if (origReq.includes("Ice Stone") || origReq.includes("/")) {
      evolvesTo = [
        {
          ...evolvesTo[0],
          displayName: "Sandslash",
          speciesName: "sandslash",
          evolutionRequirement: "Level 22"
        },
        {
          ...evolvesTo[0],
          displayName: "Alolan Sandslash",
          speciesName: "sandslash-alola",
          evolutionRequirement: "Use Ice Stone",
          customImage: "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/10102.png"
        }
      ];
    }
  }

  if (evolvesTo.length > 0 && battleForms.length > 0) {
    const megaBranchesHtml = battleForms.map((form) => {
      const isFormCurrent = form.name.toLowerCase() === currentRawName.toLowerCase();
      const primaryImg = getBattleFormImageSrc(form);
      const fallbackImg = getBaseArtworkFallback(form);

      return `
        <div class="evo-branch-col">
          <div class="evo-connector-badge">
            <span class="battle-trigger-pill">${form.trigger}</span>
            <span class="evo-arrow-char">➜</span>
          </div>
          <div class="evo-node battle-form-card ${isFormCurrent ? "active" : ""}" data-species="${form.name}">
            <div class="battle-form-tag">⚡ Mega Form</div>
            <div class="evo-avatar-wrap">
              <img src="${primaryImg}" alt="${form.displayName}" class="evo-img" loading="lazy" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallbackImg}';" />
            </div>
            <span class="evo-name">${form.displayName}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join("");

    const regularBranchesHtml = evolvesTo.map((child) => `
      <div class="evo-branch-col">
        <div class="evo-connector-badge">
          <span class="evo-trigger-pill">${child.evolutionRequirement || "Level up"}</span>
          <span class="evo-arrow-char">➜</span>
        </div>
        ${buildEvolutionTreeHtml(child, currentRawName)}
      </div>
    `).join("");

    return `
      <div class="evo-stage-node">
        <div class="evo-node-cluster">
          ${nodeCard}
        </div>
        <div class="evo-stage-branches">
          ${megaBranchesHtml}
          ${regularBranchesHtml}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  const battleFormsUnderNode = battleForms.length > 0 ? renderBattleFormsRow(battleForms, currentRawName) : "";

  const standardBranchesHtml = evolvesTo.map((child) => `
    <div class="evo-branch-col">
      <div class="evo-connector-badge">
        <span class="evo-trigger-pill">${child.evolutionRequirement || "Level up"}</span>
        <span class="evo-arrow-char">➜</span>
      </div>
      ${buildEvolutionTreeHtml(child, currentRawName)}
    </div>
  `).join("");

  return `
    <div class="evo-stage-node">
      <div class="evo-node-cluster">
        ${nodeCard}
        ${battleFormsUnderNode}
      </div>
      ${standardBranchesHtml ? `<div class="evo-stage-branches">${standardBranchesHtml}</div>` : ""}
    </div>
  `;
}

export function getFilteredMoves(pokemon, selectedVersion, methodCategory) {
  if (!pokemon.moves || pokemon.moves.length === 0) return [];

  const matchedGameObj = GAME_VERSIONS.find((g) => g.value === selectedVersion);
  const targetGroups = matchedGameObj ? matchedGameObj.moveGroups : [selectedVersion];

  return pokemon.moves
    .map((m) => {
      let detail = m.versions.find((v) => targetGroups.includes(v.versionGroup));

      if (!detail && m.versions.length > 0) {
        detail = m.versions[m.versions.length - 1];
      }

      if (!detail) return null;

      let category = "level-up";
      if (detail.learnMethod === "machine") category = "machine";
      else if (detail.learnMethod === "egg") category = "egg";
      else if (detail.learnMethod === "tutor") category = "tutor";

      if (category !== methodCategory) return null;

      return {
        name: m.name,
        level: detail.levelLearned ?? null,
        method: detail.learnMethod || category,
      };
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const lvlA = typeof a.level === "number" ? a.level : 999;
      const lvlB = typeof b.level === "number" ? b.level : 999;
      return lvlA - lvlB;
    });
}

export function getPokemonFlavorText(pokemon, selectedVersion) {
  if (!pokemon.flavorTextEntries || pokemon.flavorTextEntries.length === 0) {
    return pokemon.description || "No Pokédex description available.";
  }

  const matchedGame = GAME_VERSIONS.find((g) => g.value === selectedVersion);
  if (matchedGame) {
    const found = pokemon.flavorTextEntries.find((entry) =>
      matchedGame.flavorVersions.includes(entry.version)
    );
    if (found) return found.flavorText;
  }

  return pokemon.flavorTextEntries[pokemon.flavorTextEntries.length - 1].flavorText;
}

export function renderMoveTableRows(moves, category) {
  if (!moves || moves.length === 0) {
    return `<tr><td colspan="3" class="empty-note">No moves found for this category in the selected generation.</td></tr>`;
  }

  const formatMethodLabel = (method) => {
    if (method === "level-up") return "Level Up";
    if (method === "machine") return "TM / HM";
    if (method === "egg") return "Egg Move";
    if (method === "tutor") return "Move Tutor";
    return formatPokemonName(method);
  };

  return moves
    .map((m) => {
      let levelText = "-";
      if (category === "level-up") {
        levelText = m.level === 0 ? "Evo" : (m.level ?? "-");
      }

      return `
        <tr class="move-row">
          <td class="move-level">${levelText}</td>
          <td class="move-name">${m.name}</td>
          <td class="move-type"><span class="badge-subtle badge-method-${m.method}">${formatMethodLabel(m.method)}</span></td>
        </tr>
      `;
    })
    .join("");
}

export function renderPokemonModal(pokemon, containerElement, selectedGame = "scarlet-violet") {
  const formattedId = `#${String(pokemon.id).padStart(3, "0")}`;
  const typeBadges = pokemon.types
    .map((type) => `<span class="type-badge type-${type}">${type}</span>`)
    .join("");

  const currentFlavorText = getPokemonFlavorText(pokemon, selectedGame);

  const { weaknesses, resistances } = getTypeMatchups(pokemon.types);
  const formatMultiplier = (m) => (m === 4 ? "4×" : m === 2 ? "2×" : m === 0.5 ? "½×" : m === 0.25 ? "¼×" : m === 0 ? "0×" : `${m}×`);

  const weaknessBadgesHtml = weaknesses.length > 0
    ? weaknesses.map((w) => `
        <div class="matchup-pill weak">
          <span class="type-dot type-${w.type}"></span>
          <span class="pill-name">${w.type}</span>
          <span class="pill-multiplier">${formatMultiplier(w.multiplier)}</span>
        </div>
      `).join("")
    : `<span class="none-text">None</span>`;

  const resistanceBadgesHtml = resistances.length > 0
    ? resistances.map((r) => `
        <div class="matchup-pill resist">
          <span class="type-dot type-${r.type}"></span>
          <span class="pill-name">${r.type}</span>
          <span class="pill-multiplier">${formatMultiplier(r.multiplier)}</span>
        </div>
      `).join("")
    : `<span class="none-text">None</span>`;

  const abilitiesHtml = (pokemon.abilities || [])
    .map(
      (a) => `
      <div class="ability-card ${a.isHidden ? 'hidden-ability' : ''}">
        <div class="ability-title-row">
          <span class="ability-name">${a.name}</span>
          <span class="ability-badge">${a.isHidden ? 'Hidden' : 'Standard'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `
    )
    .join("");

  const statRowsHtml = [
    { label: "HP", key: "hp", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #ef4444, #f87171)", val: pokemon.stats.hp, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.hp },
    { label: "Attack", key: "atk", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #f97316, #fb923c)", val: pokemon.stats.attack, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.attack },
    { label: "Defense", key: "def", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #eab308, #facc15)", val: pokemon.stats.defense, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.defense },
    { label: "Sp. Atk", key: "spatk", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #3b82f6, #60a5fa)", val: pokemon.stats.spAtk, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.spAtk },
    { label: "Sp. Def", key: "spdef", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #10b981, #34d399)", val: pokemon.stats.spDef, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.spDef },
    { label: "Speed", key: "spd", gradient: "linear-gradient(90deg, #ec4899, #f472b6)", val: pokemon.stats.speed, bounds: pokemon.statBounds.speed },
  ]
    .map((s) => {
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((s.val / 255) * 100));
      return `
      <div class="stat-calc-row">
        <span class="stat-name">${s.label}</span>
        <strong class="stat-val">${s.val}</strong>
        <div class="stat-meter-track">
          <div class="stat-meter-bar stat-${s.key}" style="width: ${pct}%; background: ${s.gradient};"></div>
        </div>
        <span class="stat-bound min">${s.bounds.min}</span>
        <span class="stat-bound max">${s.bounds.max}</span>
      </div>
    `;
    })
    .join("");

  const bst = Object.values(pokemon.stats).reduce((acc, c) => acc + c, 0);

  const varietiesHtml = (pokemon.varieties || [])
    .map(
      (v) => `
      <div class="variety-card-item ${v.rawName === pokemon.rawName ? 'active' : ''}" data-name="${v.rawName}">
        <div class="variety-thumb-wrap">
          <img src="${v.image}" alt="${v.name}" class="variety-thumb-img" loading="lazy" />
        </div>
        <span class="variety-card-label">${v.name}</span>
      </div>
    `
    )
    .join("");

  const evolutionChainHtml = buildEvolutionTreeHtml(pokemon.evolutionTree, pokemon.rawName, pokemon.varieties);   

  const levelUpMoves = getFilteredMoves(pokemon, selectedGame, "level-up");
  const moveRowsHtml = renderMoveTableRows(levelUpMoves, "level-up");

  let genderRatioHtml = `<span class="gender-genderless">Genderless</span>`;
  if (pokemon.genderRate !== -1) {
    const femalePct = (pokemon.genderRate / 8) * 100;
    const malePct = 100 - femalePct;
    genderRatioHtml = `
      <div class="gender-ratio-bar">
        <span class="male-text">♂ ${malePct}%</span>
        <div class="gender-track">
          <div class="gender-fill-male" style="width: ${malePct}%"></div>
          <div class="gender-fill-female" style="width: ${femalePct}%"></div>
        </div>
        <span class="female-text">♀ ${femalePct}%</span>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  const versionButtonsHtml = GAME_VERSIONS.map(
    (g) => `
      <button 
        type="button" 
        class="game-pill-btn ${g.value === selectedGame ? 'active' : ''}" 
        data-game="${g.value}">
        ${g.label}
      </button>
    `
  ).join("");

  const isCaught = isPokemonCaught(pokemon.id);
  const isFav = isPokemonFavorite(pokemon.id);
  const isInTeam = getTeam().some((p) => p.id === pokemon.id);

  const normalImg = pokemon.officialArtwork || pokemon.image;
  const shinyFallback = `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/shiny/${pokemon.id}.png`;
  const shinyImg = pokemon.shinyArtwork || shinyFallback;

  const heldItemsText =
    pokemon.heldItems && pokemon.heldItems.length > 0
      ? pokemon.heldItems
          .map((h) => (h.rarity !== null && h.rarity !== undefined ? `${h.name} (${h.rarity}%)` : h.name))
          .join(", ")
      : "None";

  containerElement.innerHTML = `
    <div class="modal-top-layout">
      <div class="modal-image-container modal-image-wrapper">
        <img 
          id="modal-primary-img" 
          src="${normalImg}" 
          data-normal="${normalImg}" 
          data-shiny="${shinyImg}" 
          alt="${pokemon.name}" 
          class="modal-image clickable-artwork" 
          title="Click to toggle Shiny form"
          tabindex="0"
          role="button"
          aria-label="Toggle Shiny artwork"
        />
      </div>

      <div class="modal-header-info">

        class="modal-action-btn team ${isInTeam ? 'active' : ''}" 
        title="${isInTeam ? 'In Team (Click to remove)' : 'Add to Team'}" 
        data-id="${pokemon.id}"
        aria-label="Team Membership">
        <i class="fa-solid ${isInTeam ? 'fa-user-check' : 'fa-user-plus'}"></i>
      </button>
    </div>
  `;

  const cryBtn = containerElement.querySelector("#modal-play-cry");
  if (cryBtn && pokemon.cry) {
    cryBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const audio = new Audio(pokemon.cry);
      audio.volume = 0.6;
      audio.play().catch((err) => console.error("Audio playback error:", err));
    });
  }
}

export function renderTeamGrid(teamList, containerElement, countElement) {
  if (countElement) {
    countElement.textContent = teamList.length;
  }

  if (teamList.length === 0) {
    containerElement.innerHTML = `
      <div class="team-empty-state">
        <p class="empty-title">Your team is empty</p>
        <p class="empty-subtitle">Tap <strong>+ Add to Team</strong> on any Pokémon card to build your roster.</p>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  const { weaknessCounts, resistanceCounts } = getTeamSynergy(teamList);

  const sharedWeaknesses = Object.entries(weaknessCounts)
    .filter(([_, count]) => count >= 2)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);

  const topResistances = Object.entries(resistanceCounts)
    .filter(([_, count]) => count >= 2)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);

  const formatPill = (type, count, isWeak) => `
    <div class="synergy-pill ${isWeak ? 'weakness-pill' : 'resistance-pill'}">
      <span class="type-dot type-${type}"></span>
      <span class="pill-name">${type}</span>
      <span class="pill-count">${count}×</span>
    </div>
  `;

  const weaknessBadges = sharedWeaknesses.length > 0
    ? sharedWeaknesses.map(([type, count]) => formatPill(type, count, true)).join("")
    : `<p class="synergy-empty-note">No shared team weaknesses ✨</p>`;

  const resistanceBadges = topResistances.length > 0
    ? topResistances.map(([type, count]) => formatPill(type, count, false)).join("")
    : `<p class="synergy-empty-note">No shared team resistances</p>`;

  const teamCardsHtml = teamList
    .map(
      (pokemon) => `
      <div class="team-card">
        <button class="remove-btn" data-id="${pokemon.id}" title="Remove ${pokemon.name}" aria-label="Remove">&times;</button>
        <div class="team-card-avatar">
          <img src="${pokemon.officialArtwork || pokemon.image}" alt="${pokemon.name}" class="team-card-image" />
        </div>
        <span class="team-card-name">${pokemon.name}</span>
      </div>
    `
    )
    .join("");

  containerElement.innerHTML = `
    <div class="team-cards-wrapper">
      ${teamCardsHtml}
    </div>

    <section class="team-synergy-container">
      <div class="synergy-header">
        <h3 class="synergy-title">Defensive Synergy</h3>
        <span class="synergy-subtitle">Analysis across 2+ squad members</span>
      </div>

      <div class="synergy-columns">
        <div class="synergy-card">
          <div class="synergy-card-header">
            <span class="status-indicator alert"></span>
            <h4>Shared Weaknesses</h4>
          </div>
          <div class="synergy-pills-wrap">
            ${weaknessBadges}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="synergy-card">
          <div class="synergy-card-header">
            <span class="status-indicator safe"></span>
            <h4>Key Resistances</h4>
          </div>
          <div class="synergy-pills-wrap">
            ${resistanceBadges}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  `;
}

import { escapeHtml } from "./utils.js";

export function renderError(message, containerElement) {
  containerElement.innerHTML = `<p class="error-message">❌ ${escapeHtml(message)}</p>`;
}

// CORE UTILITIES & BACKGROUND CONTROLLERS

function abortBackgroundOperations() {
  if (backgroundAbortController) {
    backgroundAbortController.abort();
    backgroundAbortController = null;
  }
  scrollState.loading = false;
  setSearchLoading(false);
}

function createBackgroundSignal() {
  abortBackgroundOperations();
  backgroundAbortController = new AbortController();
  return backgroundAbortController.signal;
}

function setSearchLoading(isLoading) {
  const searchWrapper = document.querySelector(".search-box-wrapper");
  if (!searchWrapper) return;

  let spinner = document.getElementById("search-input-spinner");
  if (isLoading) {
    if (!spinner) {
      spinner = document.createElement("div");
      spinner.id = "search-input-spinner";
      spinner.className = "input-spinner";
      searchWrapper.appendChild(spinner);
    }
  } else {
    if (spinner) spinner.remove();
  }
}

function debounce(func, delay = 350) {
  return (...args) => {
    setSearchLoading(true);
    clearTimeout(searchDebounceTimer);
    searchDebounceTimer = setTimeout(() => func(...args), delay);
  };
}

function showTeamToast(message) {
  let toast = document.getElementById("team-toast");
  if (!toast) {
    toast = document.createElement("div");
    toast.id = "team-toast";
    toast.className = "team-toast";
    document.body.appendChild(toast);
  }
  toast.textContent = message;
  toast.classList.add("show");
  setTimeout(() => {
    toast.classList.remove("show");
  }, 1800);
}

function triggerPokeballOpening() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById("pokeball-overlay");
  if (!overlay) return;

  setTimeout(() => {
    overlay.classList.add("open");
    setTimeout(() => {
      overlay.classList.add("opened");
    }, 800);
  }, 500);
}

// Sparkle Burst Trigger (No Glow)
function triggerShinySparkleBurst(targetContainer) {
  if (!targetContainer) return;

  const stars = ["✦", "★", "✦", "★", "✦", "★"];
  const burstWrap = document.createElement("div");
  burstWrap.className = "sparkle-burst-container";

  stars.forEach((starChar, index) => {
    const star = document.createElement("span");
    star.className = "floating-sparkle";
    star.textContent = starChar;

    const angle = (index / stars.length) * 2 * Math.PI;
    const distance = 42 + (index % 2) * 16;
    const x = Math.cos(angle) * distance;
    const y = Math.sin(angle) * distance;

    star.style.setProperty("--tx", `${x}px`);
    star.style.setProperty("--ty", `${y}px`);
    star.style.animationDelay = `${index * 45}ms`;

    burstWrap.appendChild(star);
  });

  targetContainer.appendChild(burstWrap);
  setTimeout(() => {
    burstWrap.remove();
  }, 850);
}

// ==========================================
// AUDIO & SPEECH SYNTHESIS
// ==========================================

function stopPokedexAudio() {
  if (currentActiveCry) {
    currentActiveCry.onended = null; // Unhook listener so it cannot trigger speech
    currentActiveCry.pause();
    currentActiveCry.currentTime = 0;
    currentActiveCry = null;
  }
  if ("speechSynthesis" in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  }
  updateVoiceWaveUI(false);
}

function updateVoiceWaveUI(isSpeaking) {
  const bar = document.getElementById("modal-voice-indicator");
  const label = document.getElementById("modal-voice-status");
  if (!bar || !label) return;

  if (isSpeaking) {
    bar.classList.add("speaking");
    label.textContent = "Speaking... (Click to mute)";
  } else {
    bar.classList.remove("speaking");
    label.textContent = "Play Pokédex Voice";
  }
}

function speakPokemonEntry(pokemon, explicitDescription = null) {
  // Guard: Never speak if the detail modal is closed
  if (!modalOverlay || modalOverlay.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
  if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;

  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

  const descriptionToRead = explicitDescription || getPokemonFlavorText(pokemon, currentModalGame);
  const cleanDescription = (descriptionToRead || "")
    .replace(/["\n\r\f]/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  const typesText = pokemon.types.join(" and ");
  const textToRead = `${pokemon.name}. ${pokemon.genus}. ${typesText} type. ${cleanDescription}`;

  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(textToRead);
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const selectedVoice =
    voices.find(
      (v) =>
        v.lang.startsWith("en") &&
        (v.name.includes("Google") ||
          v.name.includes("Natural") ||
          v.name.includes("Samantha"))
    ) || voices.find((v) => v.lang.startsWith("en"));

  if (selectedVoice) {
    utterance.voice = selectedVoice;
  }

  utterance.pitch = 1.15;
  utterance.rate = 1.0;

  utterance.onstart = () => updateVoiceWaveUI(true);
  utterance.onend = () => updateVoiceWaveUI(false);
  utterance.onerror = () => updateVoiceWaveUI(false);

  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

function playPokemonCryAndSpeak(pokemon, explicitDescription = null) {
  stopPokedexAudio();
  activePokemonForVoice = pokemon;

  // Snapshot the current token to prevent stale calls
  const tokenAtTrigger = modalRequestToken;

  const trySpeak = () => {
    // Verify the modal is still open and hasn't changed target
    if (
      tokenAtTrigger === modalRequestToken &&
      modalOverlay &&
      !modalOverlay.classList.contains("hidden")
    ) {
      speakPokemonEntry(pokemon, explicitDescription);
    }
  };

  if (pokemon.cry) {
    currentActiveCry = new Audio(pokemon.cry);
    currentActiveCry.volume = 0.6;

    currentActiveCry.onended = () => {
      trySpeak();
    };

    currentActiveCry.play().catch(() => {
      trySpeak();
    });
  } else {
    trySpeak();
  }
}

// ==========================================
// GRID, TEAM & FILTER CONTROLLERS
// ==========================================

async function updateTeamUI() {
  try {
    const teamList = await hydrateTeam();
    renderTeamGrid(teamList, teamGrid, teamCount);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name !== "AbortError") {
      renderTeamGrid(getTeam(), teamGrid, teamCount);
    }
  }
}

function refreshFilterIndicator() {
  if (activeFilterIndicator) {
    const hasActiveFilters =
      filterState.type1 !== "all" ||
      filterState.type2 !== "all" ||
      filterState.gen !== "all";

    if (hasActiveFilters) {
      activeFilterIndicator.classList.remove("hidden");
    } else {
      activeFilterIndicator.classList.add("hidden");
    }
  }
}

function reRenderGrid() {
  if (gridContainer) {
    gridContainer.classList.toggle("pc-box-layout", spriteDisplayMode === "pixel");
  }
  if (currentDisplayedPokemon.length > 0) {
    renderPokemonGrid(currentDisplayedPokemon, gridContainer, spriteDisplayMode, false);
    ensureGridSentinel();
  }
}

function setupCustomDropdown(dropdownId, onSelectCallback) {
  const dropdown = document.getElementById(dropdownId);
  if (!dropdown) return;

  const trigger = dropdown.querySelector(".dropdown-trigger");
  const label = dropdown.querySelector(".trigger-label");
  const options = dropdown.querySelectorAll(".dropdown-option");

  trigger.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    document.querySelectorAll(".custom-dropdown").forEach((d) => {
      if (d !== dropdown) d.classList.remove("open");
    });
    dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
  });

  options.forEach((opt) => {
    opt.addEventListener("click", () => {
      options.forEach((o) => o.classList.remove("active"));
      opt.classList.add("active");

      const value = opt.dataset.value;
      label.textContent = opt.textContent;
      dropdown.classList.remove("open");

      trigger.className = "dropdown-trigger";

      if (value !== "all" && isNaN(value)) {
        trigger.classList.add(`type-${value.toLowerCase()}`);
      }

      onSelectCallback(value);
    });
  });
}

function renderBatchRetryBanner(failedItems) {
  const existing = document.getElementById("batch-retry-banner");
  if (existing) existing.remove();

  if (!failedItems || failedItems.length === 0 || !gridContainer) return;

  const banner = document.createElement("div");
  banner.id = "batch-retry-banner";
  banner.className = "status-message";
  banner.innerHTML = `
    <span>⚠️ ${failedItems.length} Pokémon failed to load — </span>
    <button type="button" id="batch-retry-btn" class="btn-secondary" style="margin-left: 8px; cursor: pointer;">Retry</button>
  `;

  const sentinel = gridContainer.querySelector("#grid-sentinel");
  if (sentinel) {
    sentinel.insertAdjacentElement("beforebegin", banner);
  } else {
    gridContainer.appendChild(banner);
  }

  const retryBtn = banner.querySelector("#batch-retry-btn");
  if (retryBtn) {
    retryBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      banner.textContent = "Retrying failed Pokémon...";
      const signal = createBackgroundSignal();
      try {
        const { pokemonList, failed } = await fetchPokemonBatch(
          failedItems,
          0,
          failedItems.length,
          signal
        );
        if (signal.aborted) return;
        banner.remove();
        if (pokemonList.length > 0) {
          currentDisplayedPokemon = [...currentDisplayedPokemon, ...pokemonList];
          renderPokemonGrid(pokemonList, gridContainer, spriteDisplayMode, true);
          ensureGridSentinel();
        }
        if (failed.length > 0) {
          renderBatchRetryBanner(failed);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (err.name !== "AbortError") {
          renderBatchRetryBanner(failedItems);
        }
      }
    });
  }
}

async function loadScrollBatch(isFirstBatch = false) {
  if (!scrollState.active || scrollState.loading || !scrollState.hasMore) return;

  const signal = createBackgroundSignal();
  scrollState.loading = true;

  if (!isFirstBatch) {
    let scrollLoader = document.getElementById("scroll-loader");
    if (!scrollLoader) {
      scrollLoader = document.createElement("p");
      scrollLoader.id = "scroll-loader";
      scrollLoader.className = "loading-msg";
      scrollLoader.textContent = "Loading more Pokémon...";
      gridContainer.appendChild(scrollLoader);
    }
  }

  try {
    const { pokemonList, failed, nextIndex, hasMore } = await fetchPokemonBatch(
      scrollState.fullList,
      scrollState.currentIndex,
      10,
      signal
    );

    // If aborted while fetching, do not touch state or DOM
    if (signal.aborted) return;

    if (isFirstBatch) {
      currentDisplayedPokemon = [...pokemonList];
    } else {
      currentDisplayedPokemon = [...currentDisplayedPokemon, ...pokemonList];
    }

    renderPokemonGrid(pokemonList, gridContainer, spriteDisplayMode, !isFirstBatch);
    if (failed && failed.length > 0) {
      renderBatchRetryBanner(failed);
    }
    ensureGridSentinel();

    scrollState.currentIndex = nextIndex;
    scrollState.hasMore = hasMore;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError" || signal.aborted) return;
    const scrollLoader = document.getElementById("scroll-loader");
    if (scrollLoader) scrollLoader.remove();
    console.error("Scroll load error:", error);
  } finally {
    // CRITICAL: Only unlock and auto-fill if this operation was NOT aborted.
    if (!signal.aborted) {
      scrollState.loading = false;
      checkAndFillViewport();
    }
  }
}

function ensureGridSentinel() {
  if (!gridSentinel) {
    gridSentinel = document.createElement("div");
    gridSentinel.id = "grid-sentinel";
    gridSentinel.style.height = "1px";
    gridSentinel.style.gridColumn = "1 / -1";
    gridSentinel.style.pointerEvents = "none";
  }

  // Always keep sentinel at the very end of the grid container
  if (gridContainer && !gridContainer.contains(gridSentinel)) {
    gridContainer.appendChild(gridSentinel);
  }

  if (!gridObserver && gridSentinel) {
    setupGridObserver();
  }
}

async function applyCombinedFilters() {
  abortBackgroundOperations();
  const signal = createBackgroundSignal();
  refreshFilterIndicator();
  scrollState.active = false;
  gridContainer.innerHTML = `
    <div class="search-loader-container">
      <div class="pokeball-spinner"></div>
      <span class="search-loader-text">Filtering Pokédex...</span>
    </div>
  `;

  try {
    let candidateList = [];
    const rawQuery = filterState.query.trim();
    const normQuery = normalizeSearchString(rawQuery);
    const hasQuery = Boolean(normQuery);

    if (hasQuery) {
      if (pokedexDirectory.length === 0) {
        pokedexDirectory = await fetchFullPokedexDirectory(signal);
      }

      const seenNames = new Set();
      candidateList = pokedexDirectory.filter((item) => {
        const idMatch = String(item.id) === rawQuery || String(item.id) === normQuery;
        const formMatch =
          item.forms &&
          item.forms.some((tag) => normalizeSearchString(tag).includes(normQuery));
        const nameMatch = normalizeSearchString(item.name).includes(normQuery);
        const displayMatch = normalizeSearchString(item.displayName).includes(normQuery);
        const baseMatch =
          item.baseSpecies &&
          normalizeSearchString(item.baseSpecies).includes(normQuery);
        const matches = idMatch || formMatch || nameMatch || displayMatch || baseMatch;

        if (matches && !seenNames.has(item.name.toLowerCase())) {
          seenNames.add(item.name.toLowerCase());
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      });

      if (filterState.gen !== "all") {
        candidateList = candidateList.filter((item) => isItemInGenRange(item, filterState.gen));
      }

      if (filterState.type1 !== "all") {
        const type1List = await getPokemonListByType(filterState.type1, 1, false, signal);
        const type1Names = new Set(type1List.map((p) => p.name.toLowerCase()));
        candidateList = candidateList.filter((item) => type1Names.has(item.name.toLowerCase()));
      }

      if (filterState.type2 === "none") {
        const secondaryIds = await getAllSecondaryTypeIds(signal);
        candidateList = candidateList.filter((item) => !secondaryIds.has(item.id));
      } else if (filterState.type2 !== "all") {
        const type2List = await getPokemonListByType(filterState.type2, 2, false, signal);
        const type2Names = new Set(type2List.map((p) => p.name.toLowerCase()));
        candidateList = candidateList.filter((item) => type2Names.has(item.name.toLowerCase()));
      }
    } else {
      if (filterState.type1 !== "all") {
        candidateList = await getPokemonListByType(filterState.type1, 1, true, signal);

        if (filterState.type2 === "none") {
          const secondaryIds = await getAllSecondaryTypeIds(signal);
          candidateList = candidateList.filter((p) => !secondaryIds.has(p.id));
        } else if (filterState.type2 !== "all") {
          const slot2List = await getPokemonListByType(filterState.type2, 2, true, signal);
          const slot2Ids = new Set(slot2List.map((p) => p.id));
          candidateList = candidateList.filter((p) => slot2Ids.has(p.id));
        }
      } else if (filterState.type2 === "none") {
        const secondaryIds = await getAllSecondaryTypeIds(signal);
        candidateList = [];
        for (let id = 1; id <= 1025; id++) {
          if (!secondaryIds.has(id)) candidateList.push({ id });
        }
      } else if (filterState.type2 !== "all") {
        candidateList = await getPokemonListByType(filterState.type2, 2, true, signal);
      } else {
        const range = GEN_RANGES[filterState.gen] || GEN_RANGES.all;
        candidateList = [];
        for (let id = range.start; id <= range.end; id++) {
          candidateList.push({ id });
        }
      }

      if (filterState.gen !== "all" && (filterState.type1 !== "all" || filterState.type2 !== "all")) {
        candidateList = candidateList.filter((item) => {
          const pokeItem = typeof item === "object" ? { ...item, id: Number(item.id) } : Number(item);
          return isItemInGenRange(pokeItem, filterState.gen);
        });
      }
    }

    if (signal.aborted) return;

    if (candidateList.length === 0) {
      const safeQuery = escapeHtml(filterState.query);
      gridContainer.innerHTML = filterState.query
        ? `<p class="status-message">No Pokémon found matching "${safeQuery}".</p>`
        : `<p class="status-message">No matching Pokémon found.</p>`;
      if (searchStatusLive) {
        searchStatusLive.textContent = filterState.query
          ? `No Pokémon found matching "${filterState.query}".`
          : "No matching Pokémon found.";
      }
      return;
    }

    // Deduplicate candidate identifiers strictly by name/id
    const uniqueIdentifiers = [];
    const seenFinalNames = new Set();
    for (const item of candidateList) {
      const key = (typeof item === "object" ? item.name || item.id : item).toString().toLowerCase();
      if (!seenFinalNames.has(key)) {
        seenFinalNames.add(key);
        uniqueIdentifiers.push(typeof item === "object" ? item.name || item.id : item);
      }
    }

    scrollState = {
      active: true,
      fullList: uniqueIdentifiers,
      currentIndex: 0,
      loading: false,
      hasMore: true,
    };

    if (searchStatusLive) {
      searchStatusLive.textContent = `Showing ${uniqueIdentifiers.length} Pokémon`;
    }

    gridContainer.innerHTML = "";
    await loadScrollBatch(true);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError" || signal.aborted) return;
    gridContainer.innerHTML = `<p class="error-msg">❌ ${escapeHtml(error.message)}</p>`;
  } finally {
    setSearchLoading(false);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// MODAL CONTROLLERS & DATA DISPLAY
// ==========================================

async function openPokemonModal(identifier) {
  abortBackgroundOperations();

  const thisRequestToken = ++modalRequestToken;
  currentMoveCategory = "level-up";

  modalContent.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-loading-state"><p>Loading complete Pokédex data...</p></div>`;
  modalOverlay.classList.remove("hidden");

  try {
    const fullPokemon = await fetchPokemonDetail(identifier);

    // Discard stale responses or if modal was closed while fetching
    if (thisRequestToken !== modalRequestToken || modalOverlay.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      return;
    }

    currentActivePokemon = fullPokemon;
    renderPokemonModal(fullPokemon, modalContent, currentModalGame);
    playPokemonCryAndSpeak(fullPokemon);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === "AbortError") return;
    if (thisRequestToken === modalRequestToken && !modalOverlay.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      renderError(`Could not load Pokémon details: ${err.message}`, modalContent);
    }
  }
}

function updateMovepoolTable() {
  if (!currentActivePokemon) return;
  const tbody = document.getElementById("modal-moves-body");
  if (!tbody) return;

  const filteredMoves = getFilteredMoves(
    currentActivePokemon,
    currentModalGame,
    currentMoveCategory
  );

  tbody.innerHTML = renderMoveTableRows(filteredMoves, currentMoveCategory);
}

// --- Favorites Loader & Unfavorite Handler ---
async function renderFavoritesModal() {
  if (!favoritesGrid) return;
  abortBackgroundOperations();
  const signal = createBackgroundSignal();

  const favIds = getFavoriteIds();
  if (favoritesCount) favoritesCount.textContent = favIds.length;

  if (favIds.length === 0) {
    favoritesGrid.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-empty-state">No favorite Pokémon recorded yet.</div>`;
    return;
  }

  favoritesGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="loading-msg">Loading favorites...</p>`;

  try {
    const { pokemonList } = await fetchPokemonBatch(favIds, 0, favIds.length, signal);
    pokemonList.forEach((p) => fetchedCache.set(p.id, p));
    renderFavoritesGrid(pokemonList, favoritesGrid);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name !== "AbortError") {
      favoritesGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="error-msg">Failed to load favorites.</p>`;
    }
  }
}

async function renderCollectionModal(prefix, container, countElem) {
  if (!container) return;
  abortBackgroundOperations();
  const signal = createBackgroundSignal();

  const targetIds = prefix === "caught" ? getCaughtIds() : (prefix === "fav" ? getFavoriteIds() : []);
  if (countElem) countElem.textContent = targetIds.length;

  if (targetIds.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-empty-state">No Pokémon recorded yet.</div>`;
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = `<p class="loading-msg">Loading entries...</p>`;

  try {
    const { pokemonList } = await fetchPokemonBatch(targetIds, 0, targetIds.length, signal);
    pokemonList.forEach((p) => fetchedCache.set(p.id, p));
    renderPokemonGrid(pokemonList, container, spriteDisplayMode, false);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name !== "AbortError") {
      container.innerHTML = `<p class="error-msg">Failed to load entries.</p>`;
    }
  }
}

function updateCaughtProgress(totalCaught) {
  const percent = ((totalCaught / 1025) * 100).toFixed(1);
  if (caughtProgressFill) {
    caughtProgressFill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, percent)}%`;
  }
  if (caughtProgressPercent) {
    caughtProgressPercent.textContent = `${percent}%`;
  }
}

// ==========================================
// PC STORAGE BOX & DRAWER SYSTEM
// ==========================================

function closePCDrawer() {
  if (pcSlideDrawer) pcSlideDrawer.classList.remove("open");
  if (pcDrawerBackdrop) pcDrawerBackdrop.classList.add("hidden");
  if (caughtBoxGrid) {
    caughtBoxGrid.querySelectorAll(".pc-box-slot").forEach((s) => s.classList.remove("active"));
  }
  activeSelectedBoxPokemon = null;
}

function openPCDrawer(pokemon) {
  if (!pokemon) return;
  activeSelectedBoxPokemon = pokemon;

  if (pcMonId) pcMonId.textContent = `#${String(pokemon.id).padStart(3, "0")}`;
  if (pcMonName) pcMonName.textContent = pokemon.name;
  if (pcMonGenus) pcMonGenus.textContent = pokemon.genus || "Pokémon";

  const artworkSrc =
    pokemon.officialArtwork ||
    pokemon.image ||
    `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${pokemon.id}.png`;
  if (pcMonArtwork) pcMonArtwork.src = artworkSrc;

  const spriteSrc =
    pokemon.sprite ||
    `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/${pokemon.id}.png`;
  if (pcMonSprite) pcMonSprite.src = spriteSrc;

  if (pcMonTypes) {
    pcMonTypes.innerHTML = (pokemon.types || [])
      .map((t) => `<span class="type-badge type-${t}">${t}</span>`)
      .join("");
  }

  if (pcMonHp) pcMonHp.textContent = pokemon.stats?.hp || "-";
  if (pcMonAtk) pcMonAtk.textContent = pokemon.stats?.attack || "-";
  if (pcMonDef) pcMonDef.textContent = pokemon.stats?.defense || "-";

  if (pcSlideDrawer) pcSlideDrawer.classList.add("open");
  if (pcDrawerBackdrop) pcDrawerBackdrop.classList.remove("hidden");

  if (pcSummaryEmpty) pcSummaryEmpty.classList.add("hidden");
  if (pcSummaryContent) pcSummaryContent.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function filterAndSortCaughtList(list) {
  let result = [...list];

  // 1. Generation filter
  if (pcSelectedGen !== "all") {
    result = result.filter((p) => isItemInGenRange(p, pcSelectedGen));
  }

  // 2. Sort by Criteria and Direction
  result.sort((a, b) => {
    let cmp = 0;
    if (pcSortCriteria === "name") {
      cmp = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    } else if (pcSortCriteria === "type") {
      const typeA = a.types?.[0] || "";
      const typeB = b.types?.[0] || "";
      cmp = typeA.localeCompare(typeB) || a.id - b.id;
    } else {
      cmp = a.id - b.id;
    }
    return pcSortDirection === "desc" ? -cmp : cmp;
  });

  return result;
}

async function renderPCStorageBox() {
  if (!caughtBoxGrid) return;
  closePCDrawer();
  abortBackgroundOperations();
  const signal = createBackgroundSignal();

  const caughtIds = getCaughtIds();

  if (caughtCount) caughtCount.textContent = caughtIds.length;
  updateCaughtProgress(caughtIds.length);

  if (caughtIds.length === 0) {
    caughtBoxGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="status-message" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">No Pokémon caught yet.</p>`;
    if (pcSummaryContent) pcSummaryContent.classList.add("hidden");
    if (pcSummaryEmpty) pcSummaryEmpty.classList.remove("hidden");
    return;
  }

  caughtBoxGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="loading-msg" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">Loading caught Pokémon...</p>`;

  try {
    const { pokemonList } = await fetchPokemonBatch(caughtIds, 0, caughtIds.length, signal);
    pokemonList.forEach((p) => fetchedCache.set(p.id, p));

    const sortedList = filterAndSortCaughtList(pokemonList);

    if (sortedList.length === 0) {
      caughtBoxGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="status-message" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">No caught Pokémon in this generation.</p>`;
      return;
    }

    caughtBoxGrid.innerHTML = sortedList
      .map((p) => {
        const sprite =
          p.sprite ||
          `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/PokeAPI/sprites@master/sprites/pokemon/${p.id}.png`;
        return `
          <div class="pc-box-slot" data-id="${p.id}" title="${p.name} (#${p.id})">
            <img src="${sprite}" alt="${p.name}" class="pc-pixel-sprite" loading="lazy" />
          </div>
        `;
      })
      .join("");
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name !== "AbortError") {
      caughtBoxGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="error-msg" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">Error loading Pokémon.</p>`;
    }
  }
}

// SCANNER UTILITIES
// ==========================================

function closeScanner() {
  stopCameraStream();
  if (scannerModal) scannerModal.classList.add("hidden");
}

// ==========================================
// INITIALIZATION & EVENT LISTENERS
// ==========================================

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  if ("speechSynthesis" in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = () => {
      window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    };
  }

  // Set active radio based on stored sprite display mode
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="display-sprite-mode"]').forEach((radio) => {
    if (radio.value === spriteDisplayMode) radio.checked = true;
    radio.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
      spriteDisplayMode = e.target.value;
      localStorage.setItem("sprite_display_mode", spriteDisplayMode);
      reRenderGrid();
      checkAndFillViewport();
    });
  });

  if (gridContainer) {
    gridContainer.classList.toggle("pc-box-layout", spriteDisplayMode === "pixel");
  }

  updateTeamUI();
  refreshFilterIndicator();

  if (filterExpandBtn && filterDrawer) {
    filterExpandBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      filterDrawer.classList.toggle("collapsed");
      filterExpandBtn.classList.toggle("active");
    });
  }

  setupCustomDropdown("type-dropdown", (selectedType) => {
    filterState.type1 = selectedType;
    applyCombinedFilters();
  });

  setupCustomDropdown("type2-dropdown", (selectedType2) => {
    filterState.type2 = selectedType2;
    applyCombinedFilters();
  });

  setupCustomDropdown("gen-dropdown", (selectedGen) => {
    filterState.gen = selectedGen;
    applyCombinedFilters();
  });

  fetchFullPokedexDirectory().then((dir) => {
    pokedexDirectory = dir;
  });

  // Initialize Theme
  const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem("app_theme") || "default";
  applyTheme(savedTheme);

  if (themeSelector) {
    themeSelector.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
      applyTheme(e.target.value);
    });
  }

  await applyCombinedFilters();
  triggerPokeballOpening();
});

document.addEventListener("click", () => {
  document.querySelectorAll(".custom-dropdown").forEach((d) => d.classList.remove("open"));
  if (pcGenMenu) pcGenMenu.classList.add("collapsed");
  if (pcFilterMenu) pcFilterMenu.classList.add("collapsed");
});

// --- Settings Modal Listeners ---
if (settingsOpenBtn && settingsModal) {
  settingsOpenBtn.addEventListener("click", () => settingsModal.classList.remove("hidden"));
}
if (settingsCloseBtn && settingsModal) {
  settingsCloseBtn.addEventListener("click", () => settingsModal.classList.add("hidden"));
}
if (settingsModal) {
  settingsModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target === settingsModal) settingsModal.classList.add("hidden");
  });
}

// --- Main Grid Infinite Scroll ---
if (gridContainer) {
  gridContainer.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
    if (
      gridContainer.scrollTop + gridContainer.clientHeight >=
      gridContainer.scrollHeight - 350
    ) {
      loadScrollBatch(false);
    }
  });
}

// --- Live Search ---
if (searchInput) {
  searchInput.addEventListener(
    "input",
    debounce((e) => {
      filterState.query = e.target.value;
      applyCombinedFilters();
    }, 350)
  );
}

// --- Main Grid Card Event Delegations ---
if (gridContainer) {
  gridContainer.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    // 1. Caught / Identified Toggle
    const caughtBtn = e.target.closest(".caught-btn");
    if (caughtBtn) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const pokeId = Number(caughtBtn.dataset.id);
      const isCaught = togglePokemonCaught(pokeId);
      caughtBtn.classList.toggle("active", isCaught);
      caughtBtn.title = isCaught ? "Identified / Caught" : "Mark Caught";
      showTeamToast(isCaught ? "Marked as Caught! 🎯" : "Removed from Caught list");
      return;
    }

    // 2. Favorite / Love Toggle
    const favBtn = e.target.closest(".fav-btn");
    if (favBtn) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const pokeId = Number(favBtn.dataset.id);
      const isFav = togglePokemonFavorite(pokeId);
      favBtn.classList.toggle("active", isFav);
      favBtn.title = isFav ? "Favorited" : "Favorite";
      const icon = favBtn.querySelector("i");
      if (icon) {
        icon.className = isFav ? "fa-solid fa-heart" : "fa-regular fa-heart";
      }
      showTeamToast(isFav ? "Added to Favorites! ❤️" : "Removed from Favorites");
      return;
    }

    // 3. Add to Team
    if (e.target.classList.contains("add-team-btn")) {
      const pokemonId = Number(e.target.dataset.id);
      const pokemon = fetchedCache.get(pokemonId);

      if (pokemon) {
        const result = addToTeam(pokemon);
        if (result.success) {
          const card = e.target.closest(".pokemon-card");
          if (card) {
            card.classList.remove("anim-team-added");
            void card.offsetWidth;
            card.classList.add("anim-team-added");
          }
          showTeamToast(`Added ${pokemon.name.toUpperCase()} to your team!`);
          updateTeamUI();
        } else {
          showTeamToast(result.message);
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    // 4. Open Modal Card View
    const card = e.target.closest(".pokemon-card");
    if (card) {
      const pokemonId = Number(card.dataset.id);
      openPokemonModal(pokemonId);
    }
  });
}

// --- Detail Modal Interactive Delegations ---
if (modalContent) {
  modalContent.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    // 1. Direct Artwork Click Shiny Toggle & Sparkle Burst
    const primaryImg = e.target.closest("#modal-primary-img");
    if (primaryImg) {
      const isCurrentlyShiny = primaryImg.src === primaryImg.dataset.shiny;
      const targetSrc = isCurrentlyShiny ? primaryImg.dataset.normal : primaryImg.dataset.shiny;
      primaryImg.src = targetSrc;

      // Pop animation
      primaryImg.classList.remove("anim-shiny-sparkle");
      void primaryImg.offsetWidth;
      primaryImg.classList.add("anim-shiny-sparkle");

      const wrapper = primaryImg.closest(".modal-image-wrapper");
      if (wrapper) {
        triggerShinySparkleBurst(wrapper);
      }

      showTeamToast(!isCurrentlyShiny ? "Shiny form revealed! ✨" : "Standard form restored");
      return;
    }

    // 2. Fixed Bottom Action Bar: Favorite Toggle
    const favBtn = e.target.closest("#modal-fav-action");
    if (favBtn) {
      const id = Number(favBtn.dataset.id);
      const isFav = togglePokemonFavorite(id);
      favBtn.classList.toggle("active", isFav);
      favBtn.title = isFav ? "Favorited" : "Favorite";
      const icon = favBtn.querySelector("i");
      if (icon) {
        icon.className = isFav ? "fa-solid fa-heart" : "fa-regular fa-heart";
      }
      const gridCardFavBtn = document.querySelector(`.pokemon-card[data-id="${id}"] .fav-btn`);
      if (gridCardFavBtn) {
        gridCardFavBtn.classList.toggle("active", isFav);
        gridCardFavBtn.title = isFav ? "Favorited" : "Favorite";
        const gridIcon = gridCardFavBtn.querySelector("i");
        if (gridIcon) gridIcon.className = isFav ? "fa-solid fa-heart" : "fa-regular fa-heart";
      }
      showTeamToast(isFav ? "Added to Favorites! ❤️" : "Removed from Favorites");
      return;
    }

    // 3. Fixed Bottom Action Bar: Caught Toggle
    const caughtBtn = e.target.closest("#modal-caught-action");
    if (caughtBtn) {
      const id = Number(caughtBtn.dataset.id);
      const isCaught = togglePokemonCaught(id);
      caughtBtn.classList.toggle("active", isCaught);
      caughtBtn.title = isCaught ? "Identified / Caught" : "Mark Caught";
      const gridCardCaughtBtn = document.querySelector(`.pokemon-card[data-id="${id}"] .caught-btn`);
      if (gridCardCaughtBtn) {
        gridCardCaughtBtn.classList.toggle("active", isCaught);
        gridCardCaughtBtn.title = isCaught ? "Identified / Caught" : "Mark Caught";
      }
      showTeamToast(isCaught ? "Marked as Caught! 🎯" : "Removed from Caught list");
      return;
    }

    // 4. Fixed Bottom Action Bar: Add to Team / Remove from Team Toggle
    const teamBtn = e.target.closest("#modal-team-action");
    if (teamBtn && currentActivePokemon) {
      const currentTeam = getTeam();
      const inTeam = currentTeam.some((p) => p.id === currentActivePokemon.id);
      const icon = teamBtn.querySelector("i");

      if (inTeam) {
        removeFromTeam(currentActivePokemon.id);
        teamBtn.classList.remove("active");
        teamBtn.title = "Add to Team";
        if (icon) icon.className = "fa-solid fa-user-plus";
        showTeamToast(`Removed ${currentActivePokemon.name.toUpperCase()} from your team`);
      } else {
        const res = addToTeam(currentActivePokemon);
        if (res.success) {
          teamBtn.classList.add("active");
          teamBtn.title = "In Team (Click to remove)";
          if (icon) icon.className = "fa-solid fa-user-check";
          showTeamToast(`Added ${currentActivePokemon.name.toUpperCase()} to your team!`);
        } else {
          showTeamToast(res.message);
        }
      }
      updateTeamUI();
      return;
    }

    // 5. Game / Gen Selector Accordion
    const gameToggleBtn = e.target.closest("#game-selector-toggle");
    if (gameToggleBtn) {
      const drawer = modalContent.querySelector("#game-drawer");
      const targetSection = modalContent.querySelector("#game-selector-section");

      if (drawer) {
        const willOpen = drawer.classList.contains("collapsed");
        drawer.classList.toggle("collapsed");
        gameToggleBtn.classList.toggle("active", willOpen);
        gameToggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", willOpen.toString());

        if (willOpen && targetSection) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            targetSection.scrollIntoView({
              behavior: "smooth",
              block: "start",
            });
          }, 80);
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    // 6. Game Version Pill Selection
    const gamePill = e.target.closest(".game-pill-btn");
    if (gamePill) {
      modalContent.querySelectorAll(".game-pill-btn").forEach((p) => p.classList.remove("active"));
      gamePill.classList.add("active");

      currentModalGame = gamePill.dataset.game;

      if (currentActivePokemon) {
        const newFlavorText = getPokemonFlavorText(currentActivePokemon, currentModalGame);
        const flavorElem = document.getElementById("modal-flavor-text");
        if (flavorElem) {
          flavorElem.textContent = `"${newFlavorText}"`;
        }

        updateMovepoolTable();
        speakPokemonEntry(currentActivePokemon, newFlavorText);
      }
      return;
    }

    // 7. Movepool Category Tabs
    const tabBtn = e.target.closest(".tab-btn");
    if (tabBtn) {
      modalContent.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
      tabBtn.classList.add("active");
      currentMoveCategory = tabBtn.dataset.category;
      updateMovepoolTable();
      return;
    }

    // 8. Variety / Form Switcher
    const varietyCard = e.target.closest(".variety-card-item");
    if (varietyCard) {
      const targetName = varietyCard.dataset.name;
      if (targetName && (!currentActivePokemon || currentActivePokemon.rawName !== targetName)) {
        openPokemonModal(targetName);
      }
      return;
    }

    // 9. Evolution Tree Nodes
    const evoNode = e.target.closest(".evo-node");
    if (evoNode) {
      const evoTarget = evoNode.dataset.species;
      if (
        evoTarget &&
        (!currentActivePokemon ||
          (currentActivePokemon.rawName.toLowerCase() !== evoTarget.toLowerCase() &&
            currentActivePokemon.speciesName.toLowerCase() !== evoTarget.toLowerCase()))
      ) {
        openPokemonModal(evoTarget);
      }
      return;
    }

    // 10. Voice Wave Play / Stop
    const voiceBar = e.target.closest("#modal-voice-indicator");
    if (voiceBar && activePokemonForVoice) {
      if (window.speechSynthesis.speaking || (currentActiveCry && !currentActiveCry.paused)) {
        stopPokedexAudio();
      } else {
        const text = getPokemonFlavorText(activePokemonForVoice, currentModalGame);
        speakPokemonEntry(activePokemonForVoice, text);
      }
    }
  });
}

    e.target.value = ""; // Reset so identical file can be selected again

    if (scannedPokemon) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        closeScanner();
        openPokemonModal(scannedPokemon.id);
      }, 1000);
    }
  });
}

let gridObserver = null;
let gridSentinel = null;

function setupGridObserver() {
  if (gridObserver) gridObserver.disconnect();

  gridObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
    (entries) => {
      if (entries[0].isIntersecting && scrollState.active && !scrollState.loading && scrollState.hasMore) {
        loadScrollBatch(false);
      }
    },
    { root: gridContainer, rootMargin: "250px" }
  );

  if (gridSentinel) {
    gridObserver.observe(gridSentinel);
  }
}

async function checkAndFillViewport() {
  ensureGridSentinel();

  if (
    scrollState.active &&
    scrollState.hasMore &&
    !scrollState.loading &&
    gridContainer.scrollHeight <= gridContainer.clientHeight + 100
  ) {
    await loadScrollBatch(false);
  }
}

let deferredInstallPrompt = null;
const pwaInstallSection = document.getElementById("pwa-install-section");
const installBtn = document.getElementById("install-pwa-btn");

window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  deferredInstallPrompt = e;
  if (pwaInstallSection) {
    pwaInstallSection.style.display = "flex";
  }
});

if (installBtn) {
  installBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
    if (!deferredInstallPrompt) return;
    deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice;
    if (outcome === "accepted" && pwaInstallSection) {
      pwaInstallSection.style.display = "none";
    }
    deferredInstallPrompt = null;
  });
}

window.addEventListener("appinstalled", () => {
  if (pwaInstallSection) {
    pwaInstallSection.style.display = "none";
  }
  deferredInstallPrompt = null;
});

function applyTheme(themeName) {
  if (!themeName || themeName === "default") {
    document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-theme");
    localStorage.setItem("app_theme", "default");
  } else {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeName);
    localStorage.setItem("app_theme", themeName);
  }

  if (themeSelector) {
    themeSelector.value = themeName || "default";
  }
}

// ==========================================
// KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS & ACCESSIBILITY
// ==========================================
window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  // 1. "/" shortcut to focus search input (when not already typing)
  if (e.key === "/" && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
    const activeEl = document.activeElement;
    const isTyping =
      activeEl &&
      (activeEl.tagName === "INPUT" ||
        activeEl.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
        activeEl.isContentEditable);

    if (!isTyping && searchInput) {
      e.preventDefault();
      searchInput.focus();
      searchInput.select();
    }
    return;
  }

  // 2. "Escape" shortcut to dismiss topmost modal, dialog, drawer, or dropdown
  if (e.key === "Escape") {
    // Release confirmation dialog
    if (releaseConfirmDialog && !releaseConfirmDialog.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      releaseConfirmDialog.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // PC Drawer
    if (pcDrawerBackdrop && !pcDrawerBackdrop.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      closePCDrawer();
      return;
    }

    // PC Filter / Gen Menus
    if (pcGenMenu && !pcGenMenu.classList.contains("collapsed")) {
      pcGenMenu.classList.add("collapsed");
      return;
    }
    if (pcFilterMenu && !pcFilterMenu.classList.contains("collapsed")) {
      pcFilterMenu.classList.add("collapsed");
      return;
    }

    // Main Filter Drawer
    if (filterDrawer && !filterDrawer.classList.contains("collapsed")) {
      filterDrawer.classList.add("collapsed");
      return;
    }

    // Pokémon Detail Modal
    if (modalOverlay && !modalOverlay.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      stopPokedexAudio();
      if (modalContent) modalContent.scrollTop = 0;
      modalOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // Scanner Modal
    if (scannerModal && !scannerModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      closeScanner();
      return;
    }

    // Caught / PC Box Modal
    if (caughtModal && !caughtModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      closePCDrawer();
      caughtModal.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // Favorites Modal
    if (favoritesModal && !favoritesModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      favoritesModal.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // Team Modal
    if (teamModal && !teamModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      teamModal.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // Minigame Modal
    if (gameModal && !gameModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      gameModal.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }

    // Settings Modal
    if (settingsModal && !settingsModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
      settingsModal.classList.add("hidden");
      return;
    }
  }
});
